   100	### Data Files (`HipparcosData\`) - Source/Working Folder
   101	This folder contains the source HYG catalog data and Python tools for conversion. These are **working files** for the repo - finished catalogs are deployed to `GameData\CinematicShaders\PluginData\StarCatalogs\`.
   102	
   103	**HYG Catalog Files:**
   104	- `hyg_v42.bin` - Full HYG v42 catalog (~109,400 stars)
   105	- `hyg_v42_20k.bin`, `hyg_v42_50k.bin`, `hyg_v42_80k.bin` - Filtered subsets (brightest N stars)
   106	- Matching `.json` files for each `.bin` - Names and constellation lines
   107	- `hyg_v42csv\hyg_v42.csv` - Source HYG v42 catalog CSV (download from [astronexus/HYG-Database](https://github.com/astronexus/HYG-Database))
   108	
   109	**Python Tools (`Tools\`):**
   110	The following scripts are used to convert the HYG CSV into the binary catalog format used by the mod. See `Tools\README.md` for complete documentation.
   111	
   112	**Essential Scripts:**
   113	| Script | Purpose | Run Order |
   114	|--------|---------|-----------|
   115	| `convert_hyg.py` | Main conversion script. Reads `hyg_v42.csv`, generates all `.bin` files with **Version 6 format**. Sets `IsProcedural=false` for HYG real-sky catalogs. | **1st** - Always run this first |
   116	| `generate_catalog_jsons.py` | Creates matching `.json` files for each `.bin`. Reads Stellarium constellation data, filters to stars present in each catalog subset. | **2nd** - Run after convert_hyg |
   117	
   118	**Validation Scripts:**
   119	| Script | Purpose |
   120	|--------|---------|
   121	| `validate_catalog.py` | Validates binary catalog file integrity. Usage: `python validate_catalog.py <file.bin>` |
   122	| `compare_distances.py` | Compares generated star distances to HYG real sky distances. |
   123	| `validate_generated_distances.py` | Statistical distance analysis by spectral type. |
   124	| `check_generated_fields.py` | Quick check of Version 4 fields (ID, Distance, SpectralType). |
   125	| `check_json_files.py` | Summary of all generated JSON files. |
   126	| `compare_magnitude_distribution.py` | Compares magnitude distribution between catalogs. Usage: `python compare_magnitude_distribution.py <catalog1.bin> <catalog2.bin> [reference.bin]` |
   127	
   128	### Reference Data (`StellariumReference\`)
   129	- `modern_st_index.json` - Stellarium constellation line data (IAU standard)
   130	- Used by Python scripts to build constellation visualization data
   131	- **Source:** Stellarium project - modern constellation boundaries and line connections
   132	
   133	## Architecture Overview
   134	
   135	### Three-Layer Design
   136	1. **C# Layer** - Unity interop, KSP integration, UI, settings
   137	2. **Native C++ Layer** - D3D11 resource management, compute dispatch, render callbacks
   138	3. **HLSL Shader Layer** - GPU-side compute and pixel shaders
   139	
   140	### Critical: DLL Loading Pattern
   141	All native code uses a shared DLL loading abstraction:
   142	- `DllLoader.cs` loads `CinematicShadersNative.dll` via `LoadLibrary()` once
   143	- Both `GTAONative` and `StarfieldNative` reference functions from this shared DLL
   144	- **Never modify this logic** - shared DLL state depends on single load
   145	
   146	### Module: GTAO
   147	- Deferred rendering only (requires depth + GBuffer normals)
   148	- Captures depth/normals via CommandBuffer after opaque geometry
   149	- Native compute: Hi-Z → GTAO → Filter → Composite
   150	- Render callback: `CR_GetGTAORenderEventFunc()` via `IssuePluginEvent`
   151	
   152	### Module: Starfield
   153	- Renders on GalaxyCamera (sky/scaled space camera)
   154	- **Catalog-based generation** (not per-pixel):
   155	  - CPU generates `StarData` array: position, magnitude, color, temperature, ID, distance, spectral type
   156	  - Heroes: Stars with `IsHero` flag (named/generated bright stars)
   157	  - Regular: Remaining stars, full magnitude range
   158	  - Both respect galactic density for positioning
   159	- Compute scatter: One thread per star splats to HDR texture
   160	- Pixel composite: Bloom + tonemapping to screen
   161	- **Catalog Save/Load**: Binary format with metadata header, read-only mode support
   162	- **JSON Metadata**: Each .bin has matching .json with names, constellation lines, and **x/y/z direction vectors** for named stars (used for screen-space projection without reading the binary catalog)
   163	- **Atmospheric Extinction**: Real-time dimming based on KSP atmosphere data
   164	- **Global Scene Dimming**: Automatic dimming from sun glare and planetary occlusion
   165	- **Cubemap Rendering**: Renders starfield to cubemap for KSP skybox/Parallax reflections
   166	  - 1024×1024 per face, native D3D11 rendering
   167	  - GPU injection via `Graphics.CopyTexture` (fast, no CPU readback)
   168	  - Automatic backup/restore of original skybox textures
   169	
   170	### Module: Kartographer
   171	The Kartographer is **not a standalone module**. It renders as a final additive fullscreen pass on top of the Starfield output, sharing the same GalaxyCamera hook and native state.
   172	
   173	- Rendered after both Classic and Soft HDR bloom composites
   174	- Uses its own vertex/pixel shader pair (`KartographerVS.hlsl` / `KartographerPS.hlsl`)
   175	- **256-byte constant buffer** (`KartographerParams`) updated per-frame
   176	- **Dynamic focal length** derived from GalaxyCamera vertical FOV (no longer hardcoded to 60°)
   177	- **Star selection system**: SDF-based selection circle and info box with black backing, rendered procedurally in the pixel shader
   178	- **Named star tracking**: Loads JSON sidecar, projects stars to screen space, and locks the HUD to the selected star
   179	- **Text Rendering**: Retro pixel font displays star information (name, distance, magnitude, spectral type, constellation, HIP ID) inside the selection box. Rendered via compute shader to texture, then sampled in the pixel shader
   180	- Additive blending (`SrcBlend = ONE`, `DestBlend = ONE`)
   181	- No separate Manager/Compositor — lifecycle is tied to the Starfield render event
   182	
   183	### Key Data Structures
   184	
   185	**StarData** (48 bytes, GPU-friendly, Version 4)
   186	```cpp
   187	struct StarData {
   188	    int32_t HipparcosID;    // Catalog ID (0 if procedural, 1+ for generated)
   189	    float DistancePc;       // Distance in parsecs (distance modulus model)
   190	    int32_t SpectralType;   // 0=O, 1=B, 2=A, 3=F, 4=G, 5=K, 6=M, 7=L, 255=Unknown
   191	    uint32_t Flags;         // Bit 0=IsHero (can be named/important)
   192	    float3 Direction;       // Normalized world direction
   193	    float Magnitude;        // Apparent magnitude (lower = brighter)
   194	    float3 Color;           // Blackbody-corrected RGB
   195	    float Temperature;      // Kelvin
   196	};
   197	```
   198	
   199	**Catalog Binary Format (Version 6)**
   200	- Header: 256 bytes
   201	  - Magic (4): 'STRC' (0x53545243)
   202	  - Version (2): 6
   203	  - **Flags (2)**: Bit 0=ReadOnly, Bit 1=HasCustomName, **Bit 2=IsProcedural**
   204	  - StarCount (4), HeroCount (4), GenerationSeed (4)
   205	  - GenerationParams (32): MinMag, MaxMag, MagBias, Clustering, PopBias, MainSeqStr, RedGiantFrequency, GalacticFlatness
   206	  - **Rotation X/Y/Z (12)**: Catalog orientation in degrees (offset 52)
   207	  - DisplayName (64 bytes, UTF-8)
   208	  - Date (32 bytes, ISO-8601)
   209	  - Reserved (96 bytes)
   210	- StarData array: 48 bytes × StarCount
   211	
   212	**Catalog Types (Version 6+)**
   213	- **Procedural**: `IsProcedural=true` - Generated by algorithm, all sliders affect generation
   214	- **Intentional**: `IsProcedural=false` - Real sky (HYG) or curated catalogs, only rotation editable
   215	
   216	**KartographerParams** (256 bytes, constant buffer)
   217	```cpp
   218	struct KartographerParams {
   219	    // Base grid params (64 bytes)
   220	    float ResolutionX;              // offset 0

   222	    float Time;                     // offset 8
   223	    float GridIntensity;            // offset 12
   224	    float GridThickness;            // offset 16

   229	    float PreRotationYaw;           // offset 36

   231	    int GridSizePreset;             // offset 44

   233	    float _pad1;                    // offset 52

   235	    float _padAlignCamera;          // offset 60
   236	    
   237	    // Camera basis (48 bytes)
   238	    float3 CameraRight;             // offset 64
   239	    float _pad3;

   244	    
   245	    // Debug shapes / Info box (32 bytes)
   246	    int DebugShapesEnabled;         // offset 112

   283	
   284	## Text Rendering System
   285	
   286	The Kartographer includes a complete text rendering pipeline for displaying star information in the selection HUD:
   287	
   288	### Architecture
   289	```
   290	C# (KartographerSelector) → Native TextSystem → Compute Shader → RenderTexture → Pixel Shader
   291	```
   292	
   293	### Components
   294	
   295	**C# Side (`KartographerSelector.cs`):**
   296	- Initializes TextSystem with `Ac437_Rainbow100_re_66.ttf` retro pixel font
   297	- Builds formatted text: NAME, DISTANCE, MAGNITUDE, TYPE, CONSTELLATION, HIP ID
   298	- Creates 2048x1024 RenderTexture for text output
   299	- Calls `CR_TextDispatch()` to render via compute shader
   300	
   301	**Native Side (`TextSystem.cpp`):**
   302	- Uses stb_truetype to rasterize glyphs to CPU-side bitmap atlas (1024x1024, R8_UNORM)
   303	- Dynamic glyph packing - only rasterizes glyphs as needed
   304	- Layout engine with kerning support via `stbtt_GetCodepointKernAdvance`
   305	- `GlyphInstance` struct tracks per-glyph: position, size, UV rect, color
   306	- Compute shader (`KartographerTextCS`) renders glyphs to output texture via UAV
   307	
   308	**Shader Side (`KartographerPS.hlsl`):**
   309	- Samples text texture at register `t2` with point filtering (crisp pixels)
   310	- Renders inside the info box below the selection circle
   311	- Y-coordinate flip handles texture vs shader UV space difference
   312	
   313	### Font
   314	- **Face**: DEC Rainbow 100 (Ac437_Rainbow100_re_66)
   315	- **Style**: Retro pixel/bitmap aesthetic
   316	- **Rendering**: Point-sampled for authentic pixelated look (no linear filtering)
   317	
   318	## Generated Catalog Data Model
   319	
   320	### Procedural Star Generation
   321	- **Sequential IDs**: 1, 2, 3... (distinguishable from HYG which uses actual Hipparcos numbers)
   322	- **Distance Model**: Distance modulus with luminosity classes
   323	  - 90% Main sequence (Dwarfs)
   324	  - 9% Giants  
   325	  - 1% Supergiants
   326	  - Formula: `d = 10^((m - M + 5) / 5)` parsecs
   327	- **Spectral Types**: Derived from generated temperature
   328	- **IsHero Flag**: Set for first N stars (user-configurable hero count)
   329	- **Main Sequence Strength**: Controls realism enforcement (0.0-1.0)
   330	  - **1.0 (Strict)**: Bright stars forced to hot spectral types (O, B, A). Prevents "impossible dwarfs" (e.g., K-type star at mag -2.0)
   331	  - **0.0 (Wild West)**: Any spectral type at any magnitude (sci-fi mode)
   332	
   333	### HYG Real Sky Catalogs
   334	- Uses actual Hipparcos IDs (30000-110000 range)
   335	- Distances from parallax measurements
   336	- Spectral types from catalog
   337	- IsHero flag set for stars with proper names (~350 stars)
   338	
   339	## Bloom System
   340	
   341	The Starfield features two distinct bloom modes selectable at runtime:
   342	
   343	### Classic Bloom (Original)
   344	- **Algorithm**: Separable Gaussian blur on thresholded HDR values
   345	- **Pass 2 Shader**: `StarfieldPass2.hlsl`
   346	- **Characteristics**: "Spiky" star appearance with 4-spike bloom
   347	- **Performance**: Single-pass composite
   348	
   349	### Soft HDR Bloom (New)
   350	- **Algorithm**: Multi-pass Gaussian blur with prefiltering
   351	- **Pipeline**: 
   352	  1. `StarfieldPrefilter.hlsl` - 4x4 tent filter + threshold + pow(0.65) compression
   353	  2. `StarfieldBlurX.hlsl` - 31-tap horizontal Gaussian blur (sigma=4.4)
   354	  3. `StarfieldBlur.hlsl` - 31-tap vertical Gaussian blur
   355	  4. `StarfieldUpscale.hlsl` - Bilinear upscale from quarter to half resolution
   356	  5. `StarfieldPass2Soft.hlsl` - Final composite
   357	- **Characteristics**: Softer, more diffuse bloom; better for large bright stars
   358	- **Performance**: ~4-5 draw calls vs 1 for Classic
   359	
   360	**Settings:**
   361	- `UseSoftBloom` (bool) - Toggle between modes
   362	- UI: "Bloom Mode" with "Classic (Spiky)" / "Soft HDR" buttons
   363	
   364	## Atmospheric Extinction
   365	
   366	Real-time atmospheric scattering affects star visibility based on:
   367	- **Altitude**: Higher = less extinction
   368	- **Viewing angle**: Horizon has more extinction than zenith
   369	- **Atmospheric density**: Based on KSP body atmosphere parameters
   370	
   371	**Parameters passed to shader:**
   372	- `ExtinctionZenith` - Visibility multiplier looking straight up (0-1)
   373	- `ExtinctionHorizon` - Visibility multiplier at horizon (0-1)
   374	- `AtmosphereUp` - World-space up vector for extinction calculations
   375	
   376	**Implementation:** `AtmosphericScatteringData.cs` captures from KSP, passed via `CR_StarfieldSetCameraMatrices()`
   377	
   378	## Global Scene Dimming
   379	
   380	Automatic starfield dimming based on scene conditions, applied after bloom.
   381	
   382	### Sun Glare Dimming
   383	- Triggers when looking near the sun
   384	- Uses `GalaxyCubeControl.glareFade` (private field via reflection)
   385	- **Formula**: `dimming = max(1.0 - pow(glare, 0.3), 0.1)`
   386	- Minimum brightness: 10%
   387	
   388	### Planetary Dimming (Flight Only)
   389	- Triggers when planets/moons fill significant screen area
   390	- Considers: angular size, phase angle, view alignment
   391	- Early-out for bodies > 1 million km away
   392	- **Minimum brightness**: 25%
   393	
   394	### Native Integration
   395	- `CR_StarfieldSetDimming(sunGlare, planetary)` - Called per-frame from `StarfieldCompositor`
   396	- `GlobalDimming = min(SunGlareDimming, PlanetaryDimming)` calculated CPU-side
   397	- Applied in both Classic and Soft HDR pixel shaders
   398	
   399	## Cubemap Rendering for Reflections
   400	
   401	The Starfield can render to a cubemap that replaces KSP's default skybox, enabling accurate starfield reflections in the Parallax mod (and other reflection-based shaders).
   402	
   403	### Architecture
   404	
   405	**Three-Layer Pipeline:**
   406	1. **C# Trigger** (`CubemapGenerationScheduler`) - Decides when to generate
   407	2. **C# Renderer** (`StarfieldCubemapRenderer`) - Creates RenderTextures, calls native
   408	3. **Native Injection** (`KSPCubemapInjector`) - Injects into KSP's GalaxyCubeControl
   409	
   410	### Rendering Flow
   411	
   412	```
   413	StarfieldCubemapRenderer.RenderAndInjectCubemap()
   414	├── Create 6 RenderTextures (1024×1024, ARGB32)
   415	├── Native: CR_RenderStarfieldCubemap(faceTextures[], 1024)
   416	│   └── Renders each face with 90° FOV, 1:1 aspect
   417	│   └── Orientations: +X, -X, +Y, -Y, +Z, -Z (verified with guide stars)
   418	├── KSPCubemapInjector.InjectFromRenderTextures()
   419	│   ├── Backup original skybox textures (once)
   420	│   ├── Graphics.CopyTexture(rt → Texture2D) for each face
   421	│   ├── Apply to GalaxyCubeControl renderers (XP/XN/YP/YN/ZP/ZN)
   422	│   └── TriggerParallaxReextraction() - Reset SkyboxControl.alreadyGenerated
   423	└── Cleanup RenderTextures
   424	```
   425	
   426	### Performance
   427	
   428	| Stage | Time |
   429	|-------|------|
   430	| Native render (6 faces) | ~30ms |
   431	| GPU injection (6 faces) | ~3ms |
   432	| **Total** | **~33ms** |
   433	
   434	GPU injection uses `Graphics.CopyTexture` instead of `ReadPixels`/`GetPixels`, avoiding CPU readback stall.
   435	
   436	### Trigger System
   437	
   438	Cubemap generation is triggered by:
   439	
   440	| Trigger | Source |
   441	|---------|--------|
   442	| Catalog loaded | `StarCatalogManager` after successful load |
   443	| Catalog saved | `StarCatalogManager` after save to disk |
   444	| Catalog generated | After procedural generation completes |
   445	| UI closed | `CinematicShadersWindow` when window hidden |
   446	| Scene load | `OnLevelWasLoaded` if pending update queued |
   447	
   448	The scheduler queues updates during invalid game states (loading screens) and processes them on scene load.
   449	
   450	### Parallax Integration
   451	
   452	**Reflection Capture Flow:**
   453	1. Starfield renders cubemap → injects into GalaxyCubeControl
   454	2. `TriggerParallaxReextraction()` resets `SkyboxControl.alreadyGenerated = false`
   455	3. Parallax detects this immediately (within the same frame/update cycle)
   456	4. Parallax re-extracts skybox → reflection probes update in real-time
   457	
   458	**Verified behavior**: Switching catalogs or toggling starfield on/off shows immediate changes in reflective surfaces (tested on Minmus ice flats with guide stars). No scene change required!
   459	
   460	### Original Skybox Preservation
   461	
   462	When starfield is disabled:
   463	- Original GalaxyCubeControl textures are restored from backup
   464	- Parallax is triggered to re-extract the original skybox
   465	- Backup is cleared after restore
   466	
   467	### Native Export
   468	
   469	```cpp
   470	// Renders starfield to 6 cubemap faces
   471	// targetTextures: array of 6 D3D11 textures (one per face)
   472	// faceSize: resolution of each face (e.g., 1024)
   473	// Returns: 0 on success, -2 if device not ready, other non-zero on error
   474	__declspec(dllexport) int CR_RenderStarfieldCubemap(ID3D11Texture2D* targetTextures[6], int faceSize);
   475	```
   476	
   477	## Build Process
   478	
   479	### 1. Compile Shaders (whenever .hlsl changes)
   480	```powershell
   481	cd NativePlugin\shaders
   482	.\compile_Starfield.bat
   483	.\compile_GTAO.bat (if needed)
   484	```
   485	
   486	**Starfield shader compilation order:**
   487	1. StarfieldPass1.hlsl (compute)
   488	2. StarfieldPass2.hlsl (classic composite)
   489	3. StarfieldVS.hlsl (vertex)
   490	4. StarfieldPrefilter.hlsl (soft HDR)
   491	5. StarfieldBlurX.hlsl (soft HDR)
   492	6. StarfieldBlur.hlsl (soft HDR)
   493	7. StarfieldPass2Soft.hlsl (soft HDR)
   494	8. StarfieldUpscale.hlsl (soft HDR)
   495	9. KartographerVS.hlsl (vertex)
   496	10. KartographerPS.hlsl (pixel)
   497	
   498	### 2. Build Native Plugin (whenever .cpp changes)
   499	```powershell
   500	cd NativePlugin
   501	.\build_release.bat
   502	```
   503	- Output: `CinematicShadersNative.dll` → `GameData\CinematicShaders\PluginData\`
   504	
   505	### 3. Build C# Project (whenever .cs changes)
   506	
   507	**Using MSBuild (recommended for automated builds):**
   508	```powershell
   509	cd "C:\Users\Matt\source\repos\CinematicShaders"
   510	msbuild CinematicShaders.csproj -restore -p:RestorePackagesConfig=true -p:Configuration=Debug -v:m -clp:"NoSummary;NoItemAndPropertyList;ErrorsOnly"
   511	```
   512	
   513	**Build Options:**
   514	- `-p:Configuration=Debug` - Debug build with symbols
   515	- `-p:Configuration=Release` - Release build (optimized)
   516	- `-restore` - Restore NuGet packages
   517	- `-v:m` - Minimal verbosity
   518	- `-clp:"ErrorsOnly"` - Show only errors
   519	
   520	**Post-build deployment:**
   521	The project includes a post-build event that runs `copy_dll.bat` to deploy to the test folder. If this doesn't run automatically:
   522	```powershell
   523	.\copy_dll.bat "bin\Debug\CinematicShaders.dll"
   524	```
   525	
   526	### 4. Regenerate HYG Catalogs (when CSV or format changes)
   527	```powershell
   528	cd HipparcosData
   529	python Tools\convert_hyg.py -o .
   530	python Tools\generate_catalog_jsons.py -i . -o .
   531	```
   532	Then copy the resulting `.bin` and `.json` files to the game folder or commit to repo as needed.
   533	
   534	## Catalog Folder Locations
   535	- **Source/Working**: `HipparcosData\` - Contains HYG source files and Python tools
   536	- **Runtime/Game**: `GameData\CinematicShaders\PluginData\StarCatalogs\` - Where the mod loads/saves catalogs during gameplay
   537	
   538	## Settings Files
   539	- Config: `GameData\CinematicShaders\PluginData\Settings.cfg`
   540	- Log: `GameData\CinematicShaders\PluginData\CinematicShaders_Native.log`
   541	- Catalogs (runtime): `GameData\CinematicShaders\PluginData\StarCatalogs\*.bin` + `*.json`
   542	
   543	## Current Feature Set
   544	
   545	### GTAO
   546	- Ground-Truth Ambient Occlusion with horizon-based technique
   547	- Hi-Z depth pyramid for efficient ray marching
   548	- Normal-aware edge-preserving filter
   549	- Distance fade controls
   550	- Debug visualization modes (Raw AO, World/View Normals, Normal Alpha)
   551	
   552	### Starfield
   553	- Procedural star catalog with galactic structure
   554	- Hero star system with billboard PSF support (future)
   555	- Configurable: 16-1024 hero stars + regular catalog
   556	- **Main Sequence Strength control**: 0.0=wild west to 1.0=strict realism
   557	- **Red Giant Frequency**: 0.0=none to 1.0=many (was RedGiantRarity, inverted logic)
   558	- Scientific distance model: Distance modulus with luminosity class enforcement
   559	- Galactic disc + bulge modeling
   560	- **Dual Bloom Modes**: Classic (4-spike) and Soft HDR (2-pass Gaussian)
   561	- Bloom and ACES tonemapping
   562	- **Color Saturation control** (0.5-4.0): 0.5=realistic, 1.0=natural, 4.0=hyper-vivid
   563	- **Atmospheric Extinction**: Real-time dimming based on KSP atmosphere
   564	- **Global Scene Dimming**: Automatic dimming from sun glare and planetary occlusion
   565	- **Catalog Management**: Save/load custom catalogs, read-only protection, folder browser, procedural vs intentional catalog types
   566	- **Star Catalog View**: JSON-based names and constellation lines for visualization overlay
   567	- **Per-Save Persistence**: Visual rendering settings and active catalog path are saved per-save game via ScenarioModule
   568	- **Cubemap Rendering for Reflections**: Generates skybox cubemap for Parallax mod integration
   569	  - 6-face 1024×1024 cubemap rendered via native D3D11
   570	  - Injected into KSP's GalaxyCubeControl for environment reflections
   571	  - Fast GPU→GPU copy using `Graphics.CopyTexture` (~3ms total)
   572	  - Original skybox backed up and restored when starfield disabled
   573	  - Triggers: catalog load/save/generate, UI close, scene load
   574	
   575	### Kartographer
   576	- Holographic spherical grid overlay (16 meridians × 10 parallels)
   577	- Seafoam color (0.1, 0.9, 0.7) with per-line noise variation
   578	- Screen-space RGB phosphor subpixel mask
   579	- Chromatic aberration with slider control
   580	- Aspect-ratio-correct vignette
   581	- Camera-locked to starfield orientation (follows GalaxyCamera)
   582	- Yaw (-180° to 180°) and Pitch (-90° to 90°) rotation controls
   583	- Additive blending (<1ms overhead)
   584	- Rendered as final pass in both Classic and Soft HDR bloom paths
   585	- **Star Selection HUD**: SDF-based selection circle and info box with soft-edged black backing, drawn procedurally in the same fullscreen pass
   586	- **Named star tracking**: Loads JSON sidecar, projects stars to screen space, and locks the HUD to the selected star
   587	- **Text Rendering**: Retro pixel font displays star information (name, distance, magnitude, spectral type, constellation, HIP ID) inside the selection box. Rendered via compute shader to texture, then sampled in the pixel shader
   588	- **Dynamic FOV matching**: Kartographer focal length matches GalaxyCamera vertical FOV (works at any aspect ratio)
   589	
   590	## Settings Persistence Model
   591	
   592	Settings are split between global and per-save:
   593	
   594	### Global Settings (`Settings.cfg`)
   595	Stored in `GameData\CinematicShaders\PluginData\Settings.cfg`:
   596	- Generation parameters (seed, size, magnitude ranges, galactic structure)
   597	- Kartographer visual parameters (grid intensity, thickness, chromatic aberration, vignette, rotation)
   598	- These apply across all saves and the main menu
   599	
   600	### Per-Save Settings (ScenarioModule)
   601	Stored in the KSP save file via `StarfieldPerSaveSettings` ScenarioModule:
   602	- **EnableStarfield** - Whether starfield is enabled
   603	- **Visual Rendering**: Exposure, BlurPixels, BloomThreshold, BloomIntensity, ColorSaturation, **Rotation X/Y/Z**, **UseSoftBloom**
   604	- **ActiveCatalogPath** - Path to the currently loaded catalog
   605	- **IsReadOnly** - Read-only state of the active catalog
   606	
   607	This allows different saves to have different visual styles and active catalogs. Note: Rotation is applied at runtime and can be adjusted for any catalog type.
   608	
   609	## Initialization and Retry Mechanism
   610	
   611	The Starfield uses a retry mechanism similar to GTAO to handle D3D11 device initialization timing:
   612	
   613	1. **First attempt**: 0.5s after scene load
   614	2. **Second attempt**: 1.5s after scene load  
   615	3. **Third attempt**: 3.0s after scene load
   616	
   617	This prevents the "device not initialized" error that occurs when trying to generate catalogs before the graphics device is ready. The `CR_StarfieldIsDeviceReady()` native export checks if the device is initialized before attempting catalog generation.
   618	
   619	## UI Organization
   620	
   621	The Starfield UI is organized into 3 collapsible sections:
   622	
   623	### [RENDERING] (expanded by default)
   624	- Enable Procedural Starfield toggle
   625	- Exposure (-2.0 to 8.0 EV)
   626	- Star Softness (1-2 arcminutes)
   627	- Bloom Threshold (0-10 display)
   628	- Bloom Intensity (0-2)
   629	- **Bloom Mode** - Classic (Spiky) vs Soft HDR toggle
   630	
   631	### [MAIN GENERATION] (expanded by default)
   632	- Catalog Seed (0-99999)
   633	- Catalog Size (1000-100000, default 50000)
   634	- Min/Max Magnitude (-2 to 3 / 5 to 12)
   635	- Hero Count (16-1024)
   636	- **Main Sequence Strength** (0-1, default 0.8)
   637	- **Red Giant Frequency** (0-1, default 0.05)
   638	- **Color Saturation** (0.5-4.0, default 1.0)
   639	
   640	### [ADVANCED GENERATION] (collapsed by default)
   641	- **Rotation X/Y/Z** (0° to 360°) - Catalog orientation
   642	- Brightness Distribution (0.02-0.5, default 0.25)
   643	- Stellar Population (-1 to 1)
   644	- Star Clustering (0-1)
   645	- Galactic Structure (Flatness, Falloff, Band Boost, Bulge params)
   646	
   647	## Tooltip System
   648	
   649	The UI uses Unity's built-in `GUI.tooltip` system (simple and reliable):
   650	
   651	- **GUIContent with tooltip**: Sliders pass tooltip via `new GUIContent(label, tooltip)` to `GUILayout.Label()`
   652	- **Automatic hover detection**: Unity sets `GUI.tooltip` automatically when hovering GUIContent with tooltip text
   653	- **Simple DrawTooltip()**: Checks `!string.IsNullOrEmpty(GUI.tooltip)` and draws box at mouse position
   654	- **Window clamping**: Tooltips clamped to window bounds to prevent drawing outside
   655	
   656	See `StarfieldTab.DrawTooltip()` for implementation.
   657	
   658	### Kartographer UI
   659	The Kartographer tab is organized into a single collapsible Visual Settings section:
   660	
   661	- Enable Holographic Grid toggle
   662	- Grid Intensity (0.001–0.003, default 0.002)
   663	- Grid Thickness (0.00015–0.00045, default 0.0003)
   664	- Chromatic Aberration (0.002–0.006, default 0.004)
   665	- Vignette Strength (0.35–1.0, default 0.7)
   666	- Vignette Start (0.8–2.4, default 1.6)
   667	- Vignette End (1.1–3.3, default 2.2)
   668	- Rotation Yaw (-180° to 180°)
   669	- Rotation Pitch (-90° to 90°)
   670	- Reset to Defaults button
   671	- **Track Polaris** (debug toggle) - Tests named-star projection and selection HUD rendering
   672	- **Export Font Atlas** - Saves the glyph atlas to PGM file for debugging
   673	- **Export Glyph Debug** - Saves individual glyph bitmaps for inspection
   674	
   675	## UI Value Mappings
   676	
   677	| Setting | UI Display | Internal Value | Notes |
   678	|---------|------------|----------------|-------|
   679	| BlurPixels | 1.0-2.0 arcminutes | radians | UI shows arcmin, converted to rad for native (default 0.00029 rad ≈ 1.0 arcmin) |
   680	| BloomThreshold | 0.0-10.0 | 0.0-0.1 | UI ×100 for finer control |
   681	| BloomIntensity | 0.0-2.0 | 0.0-2.0 | Logarithmic: actual = display² / 2 |
   682	| ColorSaturation | 0.5-4.0 | 0.5-4.0 | Linear below 1.0, curved above |
   683	| Rotation X/Y/Z | 0°-360° | degrees | Applied at runtime, saved per-save |
   684	| UseSoftBloom | Classic/Soft | 0/1 | Classic=original 4-spike, Soft=2-pass Gaussian |
   685	
   686	## PowerShell Command Reference
   687	
   688	This project requires Windows PowerShell for build automation. PowerShell syntax differs significantly from bash.
   689	
   690	### Key Differences from Bash
   691	| Bash | PowerShell | Purpose |
   692	|------|------------|---------|
   693	| `cd path && command` | `cd path ; command` | Command separator is semicolon |
   694	| `.\script.bat` | `cmd /c script.bat` | Run batch files via cmd explicitly |
   695	| `dir /s /b *.cs` | `Get-ChildItem -Path path -Recurse -Include *.cs` | File globbing |
   696	| `>` | `\| Out-File` or `Tee-Object` | Redirection |
   697	
   698	### Build Command Templates
   699	```powershell
   700	# Compile shaders
   701	cd "C:\Users\Matt\source\repos\CinematicShaders\NativePlugin\shaders" ; .\compile_Starfield.bat
   702	
   703	# Build native plugin (run from NativePlugin directory)
   704	cd "C:\Users\Matt\source\repos\CinematicShaders\NativePlugin" ; cmd /c build_release.bat
   705	
   706	# Build C# project with msbuild
   707	cd "C:\Users\Matt\source\repos\CinematicShaders"
   708	msbuild CinematicShaders.csproj -restore -p:RestorePackagesConfig=true -p:Configuration=Debug -v:m -clp:"NoSummary;NoItemAndPropertyList;ErrorsOnly"
   709	
   710	# Regenerate HYG catalogs
   711	cd "C:\Users\Matt\source\repos\CinematicShaders\HipparcosData" ; python Tools\convert_hyg.py -o . ; python Tools\generate_catalog_jsons.py -i . -o .
   712	
   713	# Chain with error checking
   714	cd "C:\Users\Matt\source\repos\CinematicShaders\NativePlugin" ; if ($?) { cmd /c build_release.bat }
   715	```
   716	
   717	### Path Handling
   718	- Always quote paths containing spaces: `"C:\path with spaces\file.bat"`
   719	- Backslashes work without escaping in most cases
   720	- Use full absolute paths when in doubt
   721	
   722	## Rules
   723	- Never do per-frame logging, it's awful to try and sort through and almost guarantees I will miss important entries that are not logged per frame.
   724	- Comment struct members with byte alignment offsets to ensure accurate offsets and byte totals
   725	- If a .cs file is created, it must be added to the csproj before the project will compile

## Project Overview
**Cinematic Shaders** - A KSP add-on (v1.12) providing:
- **GTAO** (Ground-Truth Ambient Occlusion)
- **Starfield** (Procedural/real-sky star rendering with HDR bloom)
- **Kartographer** (Holographic grid overlay with star selection HUD)

## Environment
- C# 7.3 MAX / .NET 4.8 / Unity 2019.4.18f (LTS)
- Native plugin: C++ with D3D11 Compute Shaders

## Architecture (3-Layer)
1. **C# Layer** - Unity interop, KSP integration, UI
2. **Native C++ Layer** - D3D11 resource management, compute dispatch
3. **HLSL Shader Layer** - GPU-side shaders

## Key Data Structures
- **StarData** (48 bytes) - Position, magnitude, color, temperature, ID, distance, spectral type
- **Catalog Binary Format (Version 6)** - 256-byte header + StarData array
- **KartographerParams** (256 bytes) - Constant buffer for grid overlay rendering

## Build Process
1. Compile shaders (`compile_Starfield.bat`, etc.)
2. Build native plugin (`build_release.bat`)
3. Build C# project (`msbuild CinematicShaders.csproj`)

## Important Rules from File
- Never do per-frame logging
- Comment struct members with byte alignment offsets
- New .cs files must be added to the .csproj before compiling

    59	        // Text measurement for auto-sizing (actual bounds from native, updated when text changes)
    60	        private float _textWidthPixels = 0f;
    61	        private float _textHeightPixels = 0f;
    62	        private bool _loggedPushParams = false;  // One-time debug logging
    63	        
    64	        // Padding around text inside the box (pixels)
    65	        private static readonly float BOX_PADDING_PIXELS = 24f;
    66	        private static readonly float BOX_PADDING_BOTTOM_PIXELS = 72f;  // Extra padding on bottom
    67	
    68	        // ============================================================================
    69	
    70	        /// <summary>
    71	        /// Initialize the text system with the retro font
    72	        /// </summary>
    73	        public void InitializeTextSystem()
    74	        {
    75	            if (_textSystem != IntPtr.Zero)
    76	                return; // Already initialized
    77	
    78	            if (!StarfieldNative.IsLoaded)
    79	            {
    80	                Debug.LogWarning("[KartographerSelector] Cannot initialize text system - native DLL not loaded");
    81	                return;
    82	            }
    83	
    84	            try
    85	            {
    86	                // Build font path: ../PluginData/Fonts/Ac437_Rainbow100_re_66.ttf
    87	                // C# DLL is in Plugins/, font is in PluginData/ at mod root level
    88	                string assemblyPath = Path.GetDirectoryName(Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().Location);
    89	                string fontPath = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(assemblyPath, "..", "PluginData", "Fonts", "Ac437_Rainbow100_re_66.ttf"));
    90	
    91	                if (!File.Exists(fontPath))
    92	                {
    93	                    Debug.LogError($"[KartographerSelector] Font file not found: {fontPath}");
    94	                    return;
    95	                }
    96	
    97	                // Initialize text system with device source texture
    98	                _textSystem = StarfieldNative.CR_TextInit(
    99	                    Texture2D.whiteTexture.GetNativeTexturePtr(),
   100	                    fontPath);
   101	
   102	                if (_textSystem == IntPtr.Zero)
   103	                {
   104	                    Debug.LogError("[KartographerSelector] Failed to initialize text system");
   105	                    return;
   106	                }
   107	
   108	                Debug.Log($"[KartographerSelector] Text system initialized with font: {fontPath}");
   109	
   110	                // Create text render texture (1024x1024 for lots of room)
   111	                _textTexture = new RenderTexture(1024, 1024, 0, RenderTextureFormat.ARGB32);
   112	                _textTexture.enableRandomWrite = true;
   113	                _textTexture.Create();
   114	            }
   115	            catch (System.Exception ex)
   116	            {
   117	                Debug.LogError($"[KartographerSelector] Text system initialization failed: {ex.Message}");
   118	            }
   119	        }
   120	
   121	        /// <summary>
   122	        /// Build formatted text string for the tracked star
   123	        /// </summary>
   124	        /// <summary>
   125	        /// Get visual description for spectral type
   126	        /// </summary>
   127	        private string GetSpectralDescription(string spectralType)
   128	        {
   129	            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(spectralType) || spectralType == "?")
   130	                return "UNKNOWN";
   131	            
   132	            char type = spectralType[0];
   133	            switch (type)
   134	            {
   135	                case 'O': return "O - BLUE SUPERGIANT";
   136	                case 'B': return "B - BLUE-WHITE";
   137	                case 'A': return "A - WHITE";
   138	                case 'F': return "F - YELLOW-WHITE";
   139	                case 'G': return "G - YELLOW";
   140	                case 'K': return "L - ORANGE";
   141	                case 'M': return "M - RED GIANT";
   142	                case 'L': return "L - BROWN DWARF";
   143	                default: return "?? UNKNOWN";
   144	            }
   145	        }
   146	
   147	        /// <summary>
   148	        /// Build formatted text for star info box
   149	        /// Format: All caps, line breaks after each field
   150	        /// </summary>
   151	        private string BuildStarText(NamedStar star)
   152	        {
   153	            if (star == null)
   154	                return "";
   155	
   156	            var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
   157	            
   158	            // NAME: <name>
   159	            sb.Append("NAME: ");
   160	            sb.Append(star.Name.ToUpper());
   161	            sb.Append('\n');
   162	            
   163	            // DISTANCE: <distance> LY
   164	            if (star.DistanceLy > 0)
   165	            {
   166	                sb.Append($"DISTANCE: {star.DistanceLy:F1} LY\n");
   167	            }
   168	            else
   169	            {
   170	                sb.Append("DISTANCE: UNKNOWN\n");
   171	            }
   172	            
   173	            // MAGNITUDE: <mag>
   174	            sb.Append($"MAGNITUDE: {star.Magnitude:F2}\n");
   175	            
   176	            // TYPE: <spectral description>
   177	            string typeDesc = GetSpectralDescription(star.SpectralType);
   178	            sb.Append($"TYPE: {typeDesc}\n");
   179	            
   180	            // CONSTELLATION: <constellation name>
   181	            sb.Append($"CONSTELLATION: {star.Constellation.ToUpper()}\n");
   182	            
   183	            // HIP#####
   184	            sb.Append($"HIP{star.HipparcosID}");
   185	
   186	            return sb.ToString();
   187	        }
   188	
   189	        /// <summary>
   190	        /// Update text texture when tracked star changes
   191	        /// </summary>
   192	        private void UpdateTextTexture()
   193	        {
   194	            if (_textSystem == IntPtr.Zero)
   195	            {
   196	                InitializeTextSystem();
   197	                if (_textSystem == IntPtr.Zero)
   198	                    return;
   199	            }
   200	
   201	            // Build text for current star
   202	            string text = BuildStarText(TrackedStar);
   203	            
   204	            // Skip if text hasn't changed
   205	            if (text == _lastText && !_textDirty)
   206	                return;
   207	
   208	            _lastText = text;
   209	            _textDirty = false;
   210	
   211	            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
   212	            {
   213	                // Clear texture
   214	                RenderTexture.active = _textTexture;
   215	                GL.Clear(true, true, Color.clear);
   216	                RenderTexture.active = null;
   217	                return;
   218	            }
   219	
   220	            // Layout text in native code
   221	            uint color = 0xFFFFFFFF; // White ARGB
   222	            int glyphCount = StarfieldNative.CR_TextLayout(_textSystem, text, FONT_SIZE, color);
   223	
   224	            if (glyphCount <= 0)
   225	            {
   226	                Debug.LogWarning("[KartographerSelector] Text layout returned 0 glyphs");
   227	                return;
   228	            }
   229	            
   230	            // Get ACTUAL rendered bounds (width from glyph coverage, height from line metrics)
   231	            float measuredWidth, measuredHeight;
   232	            StarfieldNative.CR_TextMeasure(_textSystem, text, FONT_SIZE, out measuredWidth, out measuredHeight);
   233	            
   234	            float boundsWidth, boundsHeight;
   235	            StarfieldNative.CR_TextGetBounds(_textSystem, out boundsWidth, out boundsHeight);
   236	            
   237	            // Use bounds for width (actual pixel coverage including last glyph), measure for height (proper line spacing)
   238	            _textWidthPixels = boundsWidth;
   239	            _textHeightPixels = measuredHeight;
   240	            
   241	            // Log text content and dimensions for debugging
   242	            string[] lines = text.Split('\n');
   243	            int maxLineLen = 0;
   244	            string longestLine = "";
   245	            foreach (var line in lines)
   246	            {
   247	                if (line.Length > maxLineLen)
   248	                {
   249	                    maxLineLen = line.Length;
   250	                    longestLine = line;
   251	                }
   252	            }
   253	            float screenAspectLog = (float)Screen.width / Screen.height;
   254	            float p2uX = (2.0f * screenAspectLog) / Screen.width;
   255	            float p2uY = 2.0f / Screen.height;
   256	            Debug.Log($"[KartographerSelector] TEXT DEBUG:");
   257	            Debug.Log($"  Content: {text.Replace('\n', '|')}");
   258	            Debug.Log($"  Longest line ({maxLineLen} chars): '{longestLine}'");
   259	            Debug.Log($"  Measured: {measuredWidth:F1} x {measuredHeight:F1}px");
   260	            Debug.Log($"  Bounds: {boundsWidth:F1} x {boundsHeight:F1}px");
   261	            Debug.Log($"  Using: {_textWidthPixels:F1} x {_textHeightPixels:F1}px");
   262	            Debug.Log($"  Screen: {Screen.width}x{Screen.height}, pixelsToUv=({p2uX:F6}, {p2uY:F6})");
   263	
   264	            // DEBUG: Export atlas to file
   265	            string atlasPath = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "CinematicShaders_Atlas.pgm");
   266	            StarfieldNative.CR_TextExportAtlas(_textSystem, atlasPath);
   267	            Debug.Log($"[KartographerSelector] Atlas exported to: {atlasPath}");
   268	
   269	            // Get glyph data pointer from native
   270	            System.IntPtr glyphPtr = StarfieldNative.CR_TextGetGlyphPtr(_textSystem);
   271	            if (glyphPtr == System.IntPtr.Zero)
   272	            {
   273	                Debug.LogError("[KartographerSelector] Failed to get glyph pointer");
   274	                return;
   275	            }
   276	
   277	            // Ensure compute buffer is sized correctly
   278	            int bufferSize = glyphCount * System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(StarfieldNative.GlyphData));
   279	            if (_glyphBuffer == null || _glyphBuffer.count < glyphCount)
   280	            {
   281	                if (_glyphBuffer != null)
   282	                    _glyphBuffer.Release();
   283	                
   284	                _glyphBuffer = new ComputeBuffer(Mathf.Max(glyphCount, 64), System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(StarfieldNative.GlyphData)), ComputeBufferType.Default);
   285	            }
   286	
   287	            // Dispatch native compute shader to render text to texture
   288	            // The glyph buffer is created/managed internally by the text system
   289	            StarfieldNative.CR_TextDispatch(
   290	                _textSystem,
   291	                _textTexture.GetNativeTexturePtr(),
   292	                glyphCount,
   293	                1024,
   294	                1024);
   295	            
   296	            // Set text texture for pixel shader sampling
   297	            StarfieldNative.CR_SetTextTexture(_textTexture.GetNativeTexturePtr());
   298	            
   299	            Debug.Log($"[KartographerSelector] Text rendered: {glyphCount} glyphs for '{text.Replace('\n', '|')}'");
   300	        }
   301	
   302	        /// <summary>
   303	        /// Load the JSON sidecar for the active catalog using simple parsing
   304	        /// </summary>
   305	        public void LoadJsonForCatalog(string binPath)
   306	        {
   307	            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(binPath))
   308	            {
   309	                Debug.Log("[KartographerSelector] No active catalog path");
   310	                return;
   311	            }
   312	
   313	            if (_jsonLoaded && _lastCatalogPath == binPath)
   314	                return; // Already loaded
   315	
   316	            string jsonPath = Path.ChangeExtension(binPath, ".json");
   317	            if (!File.Exists(jsonPath))
   318	            {
   319	                Debug.Log($"[KartographerSelector] No JSON sidecar found: {jsonPath}");
   320	                return;
   321	            }
   322	
   323	            try
   324	            {
   325	                string json = File.ReadAllText(jsonPath);
   326	                ParseJsonStars(json);
   327	                
   328	                _jsonLoaded = true;
   329	                _lastCatalogPath = binPath;
   330	                Debug.Log($"[KartographerSelector] Loaded {_namedStars.Count} named stars from {jsonPath}");
   331	            }
   332	            catch (System.Exception ex)
   333	            {
   334	                Debug.LogError($"[KartographerSelector] Failed to load JSON: {ex.Message}");
   335	            }
   336	        }
   337	
   338	        /// <summary>
   339	        /// Simple JSON parsing for star entries - extracts HIP ID and x,y,z
   340	        /// </summary>
   341	        private void ParseJsonStars(string json)
   342	        {
   343	            _namedStars.Clear();
   344	
   345	            // Find the "stars" object in the JSON
   346	            int starsStart = json.IndexOf("\"stars\":");
   347	            if (starsStart < 0) return;
   348	
   349	            int braceStart = json.IndexOf('{', starsStart);
   350	            if (braceStart < 0) return;
   351	
   352	            // Parse each star entry: "HIP_ID": { ... }
   353	            int pos = braceStart + 1;
   354	            int depth = 1;
   355	
   356	            while (pos < json.Length && depth > 0)
   357	            {
   358	                // Find next quoted key (HIP ID)
   359	                int quoteStart = json.IndexOf('"', pos);
   360	                if (quoteStart < 0) break;
   361	
   362	                int quoteEnd = json.IndexOf('"', quoteStart + 1);
   363	                if (quoteEnd < 0) break;
   364	
   365	                string hipIdStr = json.Substring(quoteStart + 1, quoteEnd - quoteStart - 1);
   366	                if (!int.TryParse(hipIdStr, out int hipId))
   367	                {
   368	                    pos = quoteEnd + 1;
   369	                    continue;
   370	                }
   371	
   372	                // Find the star object for this HIP ID
   373	                int starBraceStart = json.IndexOf('{', quoteEnd);
   374	                if (starBraceStart < 0) break;
   375	
   376	                // Extract star properties
   377	                int starBraceEnd = FindMatchingBrace(json, starBraceStart);
   378	                if (starBraceEnd < 0) break;
   379	
   380	                string starJson = json.Substring(starBraceStart, starBraceEnd - starBraceStart + 1);
   381	                NamedStar star = ParseStarEntry(hipId, starJson);
   382	                if (star != null)
   383	                {
   384	                    _namedStars[hipId] = star;
   385	                }
   386	
   387	                pos = starBraceEnd + 1;
   388	
   389	                // Check for closing of stars object
   390	                int nextChar = pos;
   391	                while (nextChar < json.Length && char.IsWhiteSpace(json[nextChar])) nextChar++;
   392	                if (nextChar < json.Length && json[nextChar] == '}')
   393	                    break; // End of stars object
   394	            }
   395	        }
   396	
   397	        /// <summary>
   398	        /// Find the matching closing brace for an opening brace at startIndex
   399	        /// </summary>
   400	        private int FindMatchingBrace(string json, int startIndex)
   401	        {
   402	            int depth = 1;
   403	            int pos = startIndex + 1;
   404	            bool inString = false;
   405	
   406	            while (pos < json.Length && depth > 0)
   407	            {
   408	                char c = json[pos];
   409	                if (c == '"' && (pos == 0 || json[pos - 1] != '\\'))
   410	                {
   411	                    inString = !inString;
   412	                }
   413	                else if (!inString)
   414	                {
   415	                    if (c == '{') depth++;
   416	                    else if (c == '}') depth--;
   417	                }
   418	                pos++;
   419	            }
   420	
   421	            return depth == 0 ? pos - 1 : -1;
   422	        }
   423	
   424	        /// <summary>
   425	        /// Parse individual star properties from JSON snippet
   426	        /// </summary>
   427	        private NamedStar ParseStarEntry(int hipId, string starJson)
   428	        {
   429	            var star = new NamedStar
   430	            {
   431	                HipparcosID = hipId,
   432	                Name = ExtractStringValue(starJson, "proper") ?? ExtractStringValue(starJson, "full_designation") ?? $"HIP {hipId}",
   433	                SpectralType = ExtractStringValue(starJson, "spectral") ?? "?",
   434	                Magnitude = ExtractFloatValue(starJson, "magnitude", 99f),
   435	                DistanceLy = ExtractFloatValue(starJson, "distance_ly", 0f),
   436	                Constellation = ExtractStringValue(starJson, "constellation") ?? "?"
   437	            };
   438	
   439	            // Extract direction vector
   440	            float x = ExtractFloatValue(starJson, "x", 0f);
   441	            float y = ExtractFloatValue(starJson, "y", 0f);
   442	            float z = ExtractFloatValue(starJson, "z", 0f);
   443	            star.Direction = new Vector3(x, y, z).normalized;
   444	
   445	            // Only return if we got valid direction
   446	            if (star.Direction.sqrMagnitude > 0.001f)
   447	                return star;
   448	
   449	            return null;
   450	        }
   451	
   452	        private string ExtractStringValue(string json, string key)
   453	        {
   454	            string pattern = "\"" + key + "\"";
   455	            int keyPos = json.IndexOf(pattern);
   456	            if (keyPos < 0) return null;
   457	
   458	            int colonPos = json.IndexOf(':', keyPos);
   459	            if (colonPos < 0) return null;
   460	
   461	            int quoteStart = json.IndexOf('"', colonPos);
   462	            if (quoteStart < 0) return null;
   463	
   464	            int quoteEnd = json.IndexOf('"', quoteStart + 1);
   465	            if (quoteEnd < 0) return null;
   466	
   467	            return json.Substring(quoteStart + 1, quoteEnd - quoteStart - 1);
   468	        }
   469	
   470	        private float ExtractFloatValue(string json, string key, float defaultVal)
   471	        {
   472	            string pattern = "\"" + key + "\"";
   473	            int keyPos = json.IndexOf(pattern);
   474	            if (keyPos < 0) return defaultVal;
   475	
   476	            int colonPos = json.IndexOf(':', keyPos);
   477	            if (colonPos < 0) return defaultVal;
   478	
   479	            int commaPos = json.IndexOf(',', colonPos);
   480	            int bracePos = json.IndexOf('}', colonPos);
   481	
   482	            int endPos = commaPos > 0 && (bracePos < 0 || commaPos < bracePos) ? commaPos : bracePos;
   483	            if (endPos < 0) endPos = json.Length;
   484	
   485	            string valStr = json.Substring(colonPos + 1, endPos - colonPos - 1).Trim();
   486	            if (float.TryParse(valStr, System.Globalization.NumberStyles.Float, 
   487	                System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out float result))
   488	            {
   489	                return result;
   490	            }
   491	
   492	            return defaultVal;
   493	        }
   494	
   495	        /// <summary>
   496	        /// Find and start tracking a specific star by HIP ID
   497	        /// </summary>
   498	        public void TrackStarByHipId(int hipId)
   499	        {
   500	            if (!_jsonLoaded)
   501	            {
   502	                Debug.Log("[KartographerSelector] Cannot track star - JSON not loaded");
   503	                return;
   504	            }
   505	
   506	            if (_namedStars.TryGetValue(hipId, out var star))
   507	            {
   508	                TrackedStar = star;
   509	                SelectionCircleEnabled = true;
   510	                _textDirty = true; // Mark text for update
   511	                Debug.Log($"[KartographerSelector] Now tracking: {star.Name} (HIP {hipId})");
   512	            }
   513	            else
   514	            {
   515	                Debug.LogWarning($"[KartographerSelector] Star HIP {hipId} not found in named stars");
   516	            }
   517	        }
   518	
   519	        /// <summary>
   520	        /// Update projection and push to native plugin
   521	        /// Call this from StarfieldCompositor.Update() or similar
   522	        /// </summary>
   523	        public void Update()
   524	        {
   525	            if (!SelectionCircleEnabled || TrackedStar == null)
   526	            {
   527	                TrackedStarScreenUV = new Vector2(-1, -1);
   528	                PushToNative(false);
   529	                return;
   530	            }
   531	
   532	            // Apply catalog rotation to star direction (HYG catalogs are rotated to match game coords)
   533	            Vector3 rotatedDir = KartographerMath.ApplyCatalogRotation(
   534	                TrackedStar.Direction,
   535	                StarfieldSettings.RotationX,
   536	                StarfieldSettings.RotationY,
   537	                StarfieldSettings.RotationZ
   538	            );
   539	
   540	            // Project to screen space
   541	            TrackedStarScreenUV = KartographerMath.WorldDirectionToScreenUV(
   542	                rotatedDir,
   543	                CameraRight,
   544	                CameraUp,
   545	                CameraForward,
   546	                AspectRatio,
   547	                VerticalFOV
   548	            );
   549	
   550	            // Update text texture if needed
   551	            if (_textDirty || TrackedStar != null)
   552	            {
   553	                UpdateTextTexture();
   554	            }
   555	
   556	            // Push to native if on screen
   557	            bool onScreen = KartographerMath.IsOnScreen(TrackedStarScreenUV);
   558	            PushToNative(onScreen);
   559	        }
   560	
   561	        /// <summary>
   562	        /// Push selection circle and info box params to native plugin.
   563	        /// Merges with cached state so grid settings are preserved.
   564	        /// </summary>
   565	        private void PushToNative(bool visible)
   566	        {
   567	            if (!StarfieldNative.IsLoaded)
   568	                return;
   569	
   570	            // Convert [0,1] screen UV to shader-uv space where center is (0,0) and Y is up.
   571	            float u = TrackedStarScreenUV.x;
   572	            float v = TrackedStarScreenUV.y;
   573	            float centerX = (u - 0.5f) * 2.0f * AspectRatio;
   574	            float centerY = (v - 0.5f) * 2.0f;
   575	
   576	            float focalLength = VerticalFOV > 0.001f
   577	                ? 1.0f / Mathf.Tan(VerticalFOV * 0.5f)
   578	                : 1.732f;
   579	
   580	            // Box positioned below and to the right of the selection circle.
   581	            // In shader-uv: +X = right, +Y = down (input.uv.y=0 is top of screen).
   582	            // So "below" means larger Y.
   583	            float radius = 0.02f;
   584	            float boxTopLeftX = centerX + radius + radius * 0.25f;
   585	            float boxTopLeftY = centerY + radius + radius * 1.25f;
   586	
   587	            // Merge with cached params so we don't stomp grid settings
   588	            var kartParams = StarfieldNative.LastKartographerParams;
   589	            kartParams.GridIntensity = StarfieldSettings.KartographerGridIntensity;
   590	            kartParams.GridThickness = StarfieldSettings.KartographerGridThickness;
   591	            kartParams.ChromaticAberrationStrength = StarfieldSettings.KartographerCAStrength;
   592	            kartParams.VignetteStrength = StarfieldSettings.KartographerVignetteStrength;
   593	            kartParams.VignetteStart = StarfieldSettings.KartographerVignetteStart;
   594	            kartParams.VignetteEnd = StarfieldSettings.KartographerVignetteEnd;
   595	            kartParams.PreRotationYaw = StarfieldSettings.KartographerRotationYaw;
   596	            kartParams.PreRotationPitch = StarfieldSettings.KartographerRotationPitch;
   597	            kartParams.GridSizePreset = StarfieldSettings.KartographerGridSize;
   598	            kartParams.GridColorIndex = StarfieldSettings.KartographerGridColor;
   599	            kartParams.DebugShapesEnabled = 0;
   600	            kartParams.FocalLength = focalLength;
   601	            // Calculate UV conversion for 1:1 pixel mapping (square pixels)
   602	            // Shader UV space: X=[-aspect, aspect], Y=[-1, 1]
   603	            // For square pixels, use same scale factor for X and Y based on height
   604	            float pixelsToUvY = 2.0f / Screen.height;                    // base scale on height
   605	            float pixelsToUvX = pixelsToUvY;                             // same scale for square pixels
   606	            
   607	            // Box size: text bounds + padding on sides/top, extra padding on bottom
   608	            // pixelsToUv already includes aspect ratio, no extra multiply needed
   609	            float boxWidthUV = (_textWidthPixels + BOX_PADDING_PIXELS * 2) * pixelsToUvX;
   610	            float boxHeightUV = (_textHeightPixels + BOX_PADDING_PIXELS + BOX_PADDING_BOTTOM_PIXELS) * pixelsToUvY;
   611	            
   612	            // Minimum box size
   613	            boxWidthUV = Mathf.Max(boxWidthUV, 0.08f);
   614	            boxHeightUV = Mathf.Max(boxHeightUV, 0.06f);
   615	            
   616	            kartParams.DebugBoxTopLeftX = boxTopLeftX;
   617	            kartParams.DebugBoxTopLeftY = boxTopLeftY;
   618	            kartParams.DebugBoxSizeX = boxWidthUV;
   619	            kartParams.DebugBoxSizeY = boxHeightUV;
   620	            kartParams.DebugBoxThickness = 0.001f;
   621	            kartParams.SelectionCircleEnabled = visible ? 1 : 0;
   622	            kartParams.SelectionCircleCenterX = centerX;
   623	            kartParams.SelectionCircleCenterY = centerY;
   624	            kartParams.SelectionCircleT = 1.0f; // Steady (no flicker for now)
   625	            kartParams.SelectionCircleIntensity = 0.002f;
   626	            kartParams.SelectionCircleThickness = 0.001f;
   627	            kartParams.SelectionCircleRadius = 0.02f;
   628	
   629	            // Text params - TextAreaSize maps square texture pixels to shader UV
   630	            // Texture has square pixels, so no aspect ratio correction on text UV
   631	            float textWidthUV = _textWidthPixels * pixelsToUvX;
   632	            float textHeightUV = _textHeightPixels * pixelsToUvY;
   633	            
   634	            // Left-align text at top of box with padding
   635	            float textPaddingUV = 0.01f; // Small padding from edges
   636	            float textOriginX = boxTopLeftX + textPaddingUV;
   637	            float textOriginY = boxTopLeftY + textPaddingUV;
   638	            
   639	            kartParams.TextOriginX = textOriginX;
   640	            kartParams.TextOriginY = textOriginY;
   641	            // TextAreaSize matches actual text size - no stretching
   642	            kartParams.TextAreaSizeX = textWidthUV;
   643	            kartParams.TextAreaSizeY = textHeightUV;
   644	            kartParams.SelectionTextT = 1.0f;
   645	
   646	            // One-time debug logging when tracking becomes visible
   647	            if (visible && !_loggedPushParams)
   648	            {
   649	                float screenTextWidth = textWidthUV * Screen.width * 0.5f;
   650	                float screenTextHeight = textHeightUV * Screen.height * 0.5f;
   651	                float screenBoxWidth = boxWidthUV * Screen.width * 0.5f;
   652	                float screenBoxHeight = boxHeightUV * Screen.height * 0.5f;

   654	                Debug.Log($"[KartographerSelector] PUSH DEBUG (one-time):");
   655	                Debug.Log($"  Texture text: {_textWidthPixels:F0}x{_textHeightPixels:F0}px");
   656	                Debug.Log($"  UV text: {textWidthUV:F4}x{textHeightUV:F4}");
   657	                Debug.Log($"  Screen text: ~{screenTextWidth:F0}x{screenTextHeight:F0}px");
   658	                Debug.Log($"  Screen box: ~{screenBoxWidth:F0}x{screenBoxHeight:F0}px");
   659	                Debug.Log($"  Pixel ratio: {_textWidthPixels/screenTextWidth:F2}:1");
   660	                _loggedPushParams = true;

   665	            }

   667	            StarfieldNative.LastKartographerParams = kartParams;

    47	bool TextSystem::Init(ID3D11Device* device, const wchar_t* ttfPath, int atlasSize) {
    48	    if (m_initialized) {
    49	        Shutdown();
    50	    }
    51	    
    52	    if (!device || !ttfPath) {
    53	        return false;
    54	    }
    55	    
    56	    m_device = device;
    57	    device->GetImmediateContext(&m_context);
    58	    
    59	    // Load TTF file
    60	    std::ifstream file(ttfPath, std::ios::binary | std::ios::ate);
    61	    if (!file.is_open()) {
    62	        return false;
    63	    }
    64	    
    65	    std::streamsize size = file.tellg();
    66	    file.seekg(0, std::ios::beg);
    67	    
    68	    m_ttfData.resize(static_cast<size_t>(size));
    69	    if (!file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(m_ttfData.data()), size)) {
    70	        return false;
    71	    }
    72	    file.close();
    73	    
    74	    // Initialize font
    75	    m_fontInfo = new stbtt_fontinfo();
    76	    if (!stbtt_InitFont(m_fontInfo, m_ttfData.data(), stbtt_GetFontOffsetForIndex(m_ttfData.data(), 0))) {
    77	        delete m_fontInfo;
    78	        m_fontInfo = nullptr;
    79	        return false;
    80	    }
    81	    
    82	    // Get font metrics (at scale 1.0 = pixel height)
    83	    stbtt_GetFontVMetrics(m_fontInfo, &m_ascent, &m_descent, &m_lineGap);
    84	    
    85	    // Create atlas texture
    86	    m_atlasWidth = atlasSize;
    87	    m_atlasHeight = atlasSize;
    88	    m_atlasPixels.resize(m_atlasWidth * m_atlasHeight);
    89	    std::fill(m_atlasPixels.begin(), m_atlasPixels.end(), static_cast<uint8_t>(0));  // Transparent background for bitmap atlas
    90	    
    91	    D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC texDesc = {};
    92	    texDesc.Width = m_atlasWidth;
    93	    texDesc.Height = m_atlasHeight;
    94	    texDesc.MipLevels = 1;
    95	    texDesc.ArraySize = 1;
    96	    texDesc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R8_UNORM;
    97	    texDesc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
    98	    texDesc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
    99	    texDesc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_SHADER_RESOURCE;
   100	    
   101	    D3D11_SUBRESOURCE_DATA initData = {};
   102	    initData.pSysMem = m_atlasPixels.data();
   103	    initData.SysMemPitch = m_atlasWidth;
   104	    
   105	    HRESULT hr = device->CreateTexture2D(&texDesc, &initData, &m_atlasTex);
   106	    if (FAILED(hr)) {
   107	        Shutdown();
   108	        return false;
   109	    }
   110	    
   111	    // Create SRV
   112	    D3D11_SHADER_RESOURCE_VIEW_DESC srvDesc = {};
   113	    srvDesc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R8_UNORM;
   114	    srvDesc.ViewDimension = D3D11_SRV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
   115	    srvDesc.Texture2D.MipLevels = 1;
   116	    
   117	    hr = device->CreateShaderResourceView(m_atlasTex, &srvDesc, &m_atlasSRV);
   118	    if (FAILED(hr)) {
   119	        Shutdown();
   120	        return false;
   121	    }
   122	    
   123	    m_initialized = true;
   124	    return true;
   125	}

   132	    if (m_glyphBuffer) {

   146	    if (m_fontInfo) {
   147	        delete m_fontInfo;
   148	        m_fontInfo = nullptr;

   150	    if (m_context) {

   153	    }
   154	    
   155	    m_device = nullptr;
   156	    m_ttfData.clear();
