   164	void TextSystem::UpdateAtlasRegion(int x, int y, int w, int h, const uint8_t* data) {
   165	    // Defensive bounds check
   166	    if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m_atlasWidth || y >= m_atlasHeight || w <= 0 || h <= 0) {
   167	        return;
   168	    }
   169	    
   170	    // Update CPU-side atlas
   171	    for (int row = 0; row < h; row++) {
   172	        if (y + row >= m_atlasHeight) break;
   173	        int copyW = std::min(w, m_atlasWidth - x);
   174	        if (copyW <= 0) return;
   175	        std::memcpy(&m_atlasPixels[(y + row) * m_atlasWidth + x], 
   176	                    &data[row * w], 
   177	                    copyW);
   178	    }
   179	    
   180	    // Update GPU texture
   181	    D3D11_BOX box = {};
   182	    box.left = x;
   183	    box.top = y;
   184	    box.front = 0;
   185	    box.right = x + w;
   186	    box.bottom = y + h;
   187	    box.back = 1;
   188	    
   189	    m_context->UpdateSubresource(m_atlasTex, 0, &box, data, w, 0);
   190	}
   191	
   192	bool TextSystem::PackGlyph(int codepoint) {
   193	    // Check if already packed
   194	    auto it = m_glyphCache.find(codepoint);
   195	    if (it != m_glyphCache.end()) {
   196	        return true;
   197	    }
   198	
   199	    // Find glyph index (0 = .notdef fallback, which is valid)
   200	    int glyphIndex = stbtt_FindGlyphIndex(m_fontInfo, codepoint);
   201	
   202	    // Get glyph bitmap bounding box
   203	    int ix0, iy0, ix1, iy1;
   204	    stbtt_GetCodepointBitmapBox(m_fontInfo, codepoint, m_fontScale, m_fontScale, &ix0, &iy0, &ix1, &iy1);
   205	    
   206	    int bmpW = ix1 - ix0;
   207	    int bmpH = iy1 - iy0;
   208	    int xoff = ix0;
   209	    int yoff = iy0;
   210	    
   211	    // Get horizontal metrics for layout advance
   212	    int advance = 0, leftBearing = 0;
   213	    stbtt_GetGlyphHMetrics(m_fontInfo, glyphIndex, &advance, &leftBearing);
   214	    
   215	    if (bmpW <= 0 || bmpH <= 0) {
   216	        // Zero-width glyph (space, etc) - still need metrics but no bitmap
   217	        GlyphMetric metric = {};
   218	        metric.advance = advance * m_fontScale;
   219	        metric.xOffset = 0;
   220	        metric.yOffset = 0;
   221	        metric.width = 0;
   222	        metric.height = 0;
   223	        metric.u0 = metric.v0 = metric.u1 = metric.v1 = 0;
   224	        
   225	        m_glyphCache[codepoint] = metric;
   226	        return true;
   227	    }
   228	
   229	    // Find position in atlas
   230	    if (m_atlasX + bmpW > m_atlasWidth) {
   231	        // Move to next row
   232	        m_atlasX = GLYPH_PADDING;
   233	        m_atlasY += m_atlasRowHeight + GLYPH_PADDING;
   234	        m_atlasRowHeight = 0;
   235	    }
   236	
   237	    if (m_atlasY + bmpH > m_atlasHeight) {
   238	        // Atlas full
   239	        return false;
   240	    }
   241	
   242	    // Allocate temp buffer and render bitmap
   243	    std::vector<unsigned char> bitmap(bmpW * bmpH, 0);
   244	    
   245	    // Render the glyph bitmap - this fills the buffer with 8-bit coverage values
   246	    stbtt_MakeCodepointBitmap(
   247	        m_fontInfo,
   248	        bitmap.data(),
   249	        bmpW,
   250	        bmpH,
   251	        bmpW, // stride
   252	        m_fontScale,
   253	        m_fontScale,
   254	        codepoint
   255	    );
   256	
   257	    // Store metric
   258	    GlyphMetric metric = {};
   259	    metric.advance = advance * m_fontScale;
   260	    metric.leftBearing = leftBearing * m_fontScale;  // metadata only; xOffset used for placement
   261	    metric.topBearing = 0.0f;  // unused in bitmap path; yOffset from bitmap box is authoritative
   262	    metric.xOffset = static_cast<float>(xoff);  // offset to bitmap left
   263	    metric.yOffset = static_cast<float>(yoff);  // offset to bitmap top (negative)
   264	
   265	    metric.u0 = static_cast<float>(m_atlasX) / static_cast<float>(m_atlasWidth);
   266	    metric.v0 = static_cast<float>(m_atlasY) / static_cast<float>(m_atlasHeight);
   267	    metric.u1 = static_cast<float>(m_atlasX + bmpW) / static_cast<float>(m_atlasWidth);
   268	    metric.v1 = static_cast<float>(m_atlasY + bmpH) / static_cast<float>(m_atlasHeight);
   269	
   270	    metric.width = bmpW;
   271	    metric.height = bmpH;
   272	
   273	    // Upload to atlas
   274	    UpdateAtlasRegion(m_atlasX, m_atlasY, bmpW, bmpH, bitmap.data());
   275	
   276	    // Cache metric
   277	    m_glyphCache[codepoint] = metric;
   278	
   279	    // Update packing state
   280	    m_atlasX += bmpW + GLYPH_PADDING;
   281	    m_atlasRowHeight = std::max(m_atlasRowHeight, bmpH);
   282	
   283	    return true;
   284	}
   285	
   286	void TextSystem::ClearAtlasAndCache() {
   287	    m_glyphCache.clear();
   288	    m_atlasX = GLYPH_PADDING;
   289	    m_atlasY = GLYPH_PADDING;
   290	    m_atlasRowHeight = 0;
   291	    std::fill(m_atlasPixels.begin(), m_atlasPixels.end(), static_cast<uint8_t>(0));
   292	    
   293	    // Clear the D3D texture as well
   294	    if (m_context && m_atlasTex) {
   295	        m_context->UpdateSubresource(m_atlasTex, 0, nullptr, m_atlasPixels.data(), m_atlasWidth, 0);
   296	    }
   297	}
   298	
   299	int TextSystem::LayoutString(const char* text, float fontSize, uint32_t color) {
   300	    if (!m_initialized || !text) {
   301	        return 0;
   302	    }
   303	    
   304	    // Quantize font size to integer pixels (pixel fonts should use integer sizes)
   305	    int fontPx = static_cast<int>(std::round(fontSize));
   306	    
   307	    // Clear cache if font size changed (glyphs are size-specific)
   308	    if (fontPx != m_cachedFontPx) {
   309	        ClearAtlasAndCache();
   310	        m_cachedFontPx = fontPx;
   311	    }
   312	    
   313	    m_instances.clear();
   314	    
   315	    // Set font scale for this layout
   316	    m_fontScale = stbtt_ScaleForPixelHeight(m_fontInfo, static_cast<float>(fontPx));
   317	    
   318	    float cursorX = 0.0f;
   319	    float cursorY = 0.0f;
   320	    float lineHeight = (m_ascent - m_descent + m_lineGap) * m_fontScale;
   321	    
   322	    for (const char* p = text; *p; ++p) {
   323	        int codepoint = static_cast<unsigned char>(*p);
   324	        
   325	        // Handle newline
   326	        if (codepoint == '\n') {
   327	            cursorX = 0.0f;
   328	            cursorY += lineHeight;
   329	            continue;
   330	        }
   331	        
   332	        // Pack glyph into atlas
   333	        if (!PackGlyph(codepoint)) {
   334	            continue;  // Skip if can't pack
   335	        }
   336	        
   337	        const GlyphMetric& m = m_glyphCache[codepoint];
   338	        
   339	        // Create instance using bitmap positioning
   340	        GlyphInstance inst;
   341	        // xOffset/yOffset are pixel offsets from baseline to bitmap top-left
   342	        // Snap to integers for pixel-perfect rendering (critical for pixel fonts)
   343	        inst.posX = std::round(cursorX + m.xOffset);
   344	        inst.posY = std::round(cursorY + (m_ascent * m_fontScale) + m.yOffset);
   345	        
   346	        inst.sizeX = static_cast<float>(m.width);
   347	        inst.sizeY = static_cast<float>(m.height);
   348	        inst.uvX = m.u0;
   349	        inst.uvY = m.v0;
   350	        inst.uvW = m.u1 - m.u0;
   351	        inst.uvH = m.v1 - m.v0;
   352	        inst.color = color;
   353	        // No smoothing for bitmap fonts - we want crisp pixels
   354	        inst.smoothing = 0.0f;
   355	        
   356	        m_instances.push_back(inst);
   357	        
   358	        // Advance cursor by glyph advance
   359	        cursorX += m.advance;
   360	        
   361	        // Apply kerning with next character
   362	        if (*(p + 1) && *(p + 1) != '\n') {
   363	            int nextCodepoint = static_cast<unsigned char>(*(p + 1));
   364	            cursorX += stbtt_GetCodepointKernAdvance(m_fontInfo, codepoint, nextCodepoint) * m_fontScale;
   365	        }
   366	    }
   367	    
   368	    return static_cast<int>(m_instances.size());
   369	}
   370	
   371	void TextSystem::GetTextBounds(float& outWidth, float& outHeight) const {
   372	    outWidth = 0.0f;
   373	    outHeight = 0.0f;
   374	    
   375	    if (m_instances.empty()) {
   376	        return;
   377	    }
   378	    
   379	    // Find min/max bounds of all glyph instances
   380	    float minX = FLT_MAX;
   381	    float minY = FLT_MAX;
   382	    float maxX = -FLT_MAX;
   383	    float maxY = -FLT_MAX;
   384	    
   385	    for (const auto& inst : m_instances) {
   386	        minX = std::min(minX, inst.posX);
   387	        minY = std::min(minY, inst.posY);
   388	        maxX = std::max(maxX, inst.posX + inst.sizeX);
   389	        maxY = std::max(maxY, inst.posY + inst.sizeY);
   390	    }
   391	    
   392	    outWidth = maxX - minX;
   393	    outHeight = maxY - minY;
   394	}
   395	
   396	void TextSystem::MeasureString(const char* text, float fontSize, float& outWidth, float& outHeight) {
   397	    outWidth = 0.0f;
   398	    outHeight = 0.0f;
   399	    
   400	    if (!m_initialized || !text) {
   401	        return;
   402	    }
   403	    
   404	    // Quantize font size
   405	    int fontPx = static_cast<int>(std::round(fontSize));
   406	    
   407	    // Need to ensure font is set up for metrics
   408	    if (fontPx != m_cachedFontPx) {
   409	        // Just set the scale temporarily for measurement
   410	        m_fontScale = stbtt_ScaleForPixelHeight(m_fontInfo, static_cast<float>(fontPx));
   411	    } else {
   412	        m_fontScale = stbtt_ScaleForPixelHeight(m_fontInfo, static_cast<float>(fontPx));
   413	    }
   414	    
   415	    float lineHeight = (m_ascent - m_descent + m_lineGap) * m_fontScale;
   416	    float maxWidth = 0.0f;
   417	    float currentLineWidth = 0.0f;
   418	    int lineCount = 1;
   419	    
   420	    for (const char* p = text; *p; ++p) {
   421	        int codepoint = static_cast<unsigned char>(*p);
   422	        
   423	        if (codepoint == '\n') {
   424	            maxWidth = std::max(maxWidth, currentLineWidth);
   425	            currentLineWidth = 0.0f;
   426	            lineCount++;
   427	        } else {
   428	            // Ensure glyph is available for metrics
   429	            PackGlyph(codepoint);
   430	            auto it = m_glyphCache.find(codepoint);
   431	            if (it != m_glyphCache.end()) {
   432	                const GlyphMetric& m = it->second;
   433	                currentLineWidth += m.advance;
   434	                if (*(p + 1) && *(p + 1) != '\n') {
   435	                    int nextCodepoint = static_cast<unsigned char>(*(p + 1));
   436	                    currentLineWidth += stbtt_GetCodepointKernAdvance(m_fontInfo, codepoint, nextCodepoint) * m_fontScale;
   437	                }
   438	            }
   439	        }
   440	    }
   441	    maxWidth = std::max(maxWidth, currentLineWidth);
   442	    
   443	    outWidth = maxWidth;
   444	    outHeight = lineCount * lineHeight;
   445	}
   446	
   447	void TextSystem::ExportAtlasToFile(const char* filename) {
   448	    if (!m_initialized || m_atlasPixels.empty()) {
   449	        LogToFile("[Text] ExportAtlasToFile: Atlas not initialized or empty");
   450	        return;
   451	    }
   452	    
   453	    // Save as PGM (grayscale) - simple format we can open
   454	    std::ofstream file(filename, std::ios::binary);
   455	    if (!file.is_open()) {
   456	        LogToFile("[Text] ExportAtlasToFile: Failed to open %s", filename);
   457	        return;
   458	    }
   459	    
   460	    // PGM header
   461	    file << "P5\n" << m_atlasWidth << " " << m_atlasHeight << "\n255\n";
   462	    
   463	    // Write pixel data
   464	    file.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(m_atlasPixels.data()), m_atlasPixels.size());
   465	    file.close();
   466	    
   467	    LogToFile("[Text] Atlas exported to %s (%dx%d)", filename, m_atlasWidth, m_atlasHeight);
   468	}
   469	
   470	void TextSystem::ExportGlyphDebug(const char* baseFilename) {
   471	    if (!m_initialized || m_glyphCache.empty()) {
   472	        LogToFile("[Text] ExportGlyphDebug: No glyphs to export");
   473	        return;
   474	    }
   475	    
   476	    // Get first cached glyph
   477	    auto it = m_glyphCache.begin();
   478	    int codepoint = it->first;
   479	    const GlyphMetric& metric = it->second;
   480	    
   481	    LogToFile("[Text] ExportGlyphDebug: Exporting glyph %d (codepoint %d) size %dx%d", 
   482	              stbtt_FindGlyphIndex(m_fontInfo, codepoint), codepoint, metric.width, metric.height);
   483	    
   484	    // Extract glyph bitmap from atlas
   485	    if (metric.width > 0 && metric.height > 0) {
   486	        // Convert UV coordinates back to pixel coordinates
   487	        int atlasX = static_cast<int>(metric.u0 * m_atlasWidth);
   488	        int atlasY = static_cast<int>(metric.v0 * m_atlasHeight);
   489	        
   490	        // Extract glyph from atlas
   491	        std::vector<uint8_t> glyphBitmap(metric.width * metric.height);
   492	        for (int y = 0; y < metric.height; y++) {
   493	            int srcRow = atlasY + y;
   494	            int dstRow = y;
   495	            if (srcRow < m_atlasHeight) {
   496	                std::memcpy(&glyphBitmap[dstRow * metric.width],
   497	                           &m_atlasPixels[srcRow * m_atlasWidth + atlasX],
   498	                           metric.width);
   499	            }
   500	        }
   501	        
   502	        // Export glyph bitmap
   503	        char glyphName[256];
   504	        snprintf(glyphName, sizeof(glyphName), "%s_glyph.pgm", baseFilename);
   505	        std::ofstream glyphFile(glyphName, std::ios::binary);
   506	        if (glyphFile.is_open()) {
   507	            glyphFile << "P5\n" << metric.width << " " << metric.height << "\n255\n";
   508	            glyphFile.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(glyphBitmap.data()), 
   509	                           metric.width * metric.height);
   510	            glyphFile.close();
   511	            LogToFile("[Text] Glyph bitmap exported: %s (%dx%d)", 
   512	                     glyphName, metric.width, metric.height);
   513	        }
   514	    }
   515	}
   516	
   517	ID3D11Buffer* TextSystem::GetOrCreateGlyphBuffer() {
   518	    if (m_instances.empty())
   519	        return nullptr;
   520	    
   521	    int requiredCount = static_cast<int>(m_instances.size());
   522	    int instanceSize = sizeof(GlyphInstance);
   523	    int requiredSize = requiredCount * instanceSize;
   524	    
   525	    // Create or resize buffer if needed
   526	    if (!m_glyphBuffer || m_glyphBufferCapacity < requiredCount) {
   527	        if (m_glyphBuffer) {
   528	            m_glyphBuffer->Release();
   529	            m_glyphBuffer = nullptr;
   530	        }
   531	        if (m_glyphBufferSRV) {
   532	            m_glyphBufferSRV->Release();
   533	            m_glyphBufferSRV = nullptr;
   534	        }
   535	        
   536	        // Allocate with some headroom
   537	        m_glyphBufferCapacity = std::max(requiredCount * 2, 256);
   538	        
   539	        D3D11_BUFFER_DESC desc = {};
   540	        desc.ByteWidth = m_glyphBufferCapacity * instanceSize;
   541	        desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DYNAMIC;
   542	        desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_SHADER_RESOURCE;
   543	        desc.CPUAccessFlags = D3D11_CPU_ACCESS_WRITE;
   544	        desc.StructureByteStride = instanceSize;
   545	        desc.MiscFlags = D3D11_RESOURCE_MISC_BUFFER_STRUCTURED;
   546	        
   547	        HRESULT hr = m_device->CreateBuffer(&desc, nullptr, &m_glyphBuffer);
   548	        if (FAILED(hr)) {
   549	            m_glyphBufferCapacity = 0;
   550	            return nullptr;
   551	        }
   552	        
   553	        // Create SRV for the buffer
   554	        D3D11_SHADER_RESOURCE_VIEW_DESC srvDesc = {};
   555	        srvDesc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_UNKNOWN;
   556	        srvDesc.ViewDimension = D3D11_SRV_DIMENSION_BUFFER;
   557	        srvDesc.Buffer.FirstElement = 0;
   558	        srvDesc.Buffer.NumElements = m_glyphBufferCapacity;
   559	        
   560	        hr = m_device->CreateShaderResourceView(m_glyphBuffer, &srvDesc, &m_glyphBufferSRV);
   561	        if (FAILED(hr)) {
   562	            m_glyphBuffer->Release();
   563	            m_glyphBuffer = nullptr;
   564	            m_glyphBufferCapacity = 0;
   565	            return nullptr;
   566	        }
   567	    }
   568	    
   569	    // Update buffer with current glyph data
   570	    D3D11_MAPPED_SUBRESOURCE mapped;
   571	    if (SUCCEEDED(m_context->Map(m_glyphBuffer, 0, D3D11_MAP_WRITE_DISCARD, 0, &mapped))) {
   572	        memcpy(mapped.pData, m_instances.data(), requiredSize);
   573	        m_context->Unmap(m_glyphBuffer, 0);
   574	    }
   575	    
   576	    return m_glyphBuffer;
   577	}

   587	TextSystemHandle CR_TextInit(ID3D11Texture2D* deviceSourceTexture, const wchar_t* fontPath) {
   588	    LogToFile("[Text] CR_TextInit called: deviceSourceTexture=%p, fontPath=%ls", deviceSourceTexture, fontPath ? fontPath : L"(null)");
   589	    
   590	    if (!deviceSourceTexture || !fontPath) {
   591	        LogToFile("[Text] CR_TextInit FAILED: null argument (texture=%p, path=%ls)", deviceSourceTexture, fontPath ? L"valid" : L"null");
   592	        return nullptr;
   593	    }
   594	    
   595	    // Get device from texture
   596	    ID3D11Device* device = nullptr;
   597	    deviceSourceTexture->GetDevice(&device);
   598	    if (!device) {
   599	        LogToFile("[Text] CR_TextInit FAILED: GetDevice returned null");
   600	        return nullptr;
   601	    }
   602	    LogToFile("[Text] Got device from texture: %p", device);
   603	    
   604	    TextSystem* ts = new TextSystem();
   605	    LogToFile("[Text] Created TextSystem object: %p", ts);
   606	    
   607	    if (!ts->Init(device, fontPath)) {
   608	        LogToFile("[Text] CR_TextInit FAILED: TextSystem::Init failed");
   609	        delete ts;
   610	        device->Release();
   611	        return nullptr;
   612	    }
   613	    
   614	    LogToFile("[Text] CR_TextInit SUCCESS: handle=%p", ts);
   615	    device->Release();
   616	    return ts;
   617	}

   154	// Helper: Transform view-space vector to world-space
   155	float3 ViewToWorld(float3 v, float3 right, float3 up, float3 forward) {
   156	    return v.x * right - v.y * up + v.z * forward;
   157	}
   158	
   159	// Helper: Apply yaw/pitch rotation to view ray
   160	float3 ApplyPreRotation(float3 ray, float yaw, float pitch) {
   161	    float cy = cos(yaw);
   162	    float sy = sin(yaw);
   163	    float cx = cos(pitch);
   164	    float sx = sin(pitch);
   165	    
   166	    float3 r1 = float3(
   167	        ray.x * cy - ray.z * sy,
   168	        ray.y,
   169	        ray.x * sy + ray.z * cy
   170	    );
   171	    
   172	    float3 r2 = float3(
   173	        r1.x,
   174	        r1.y * cx - r1.z * sx,
   175	        r1.y * sx + r1.z * cx
   176	    );
   177	    
   178	    return normalize(r2);
   179	}
   180	
   181	// ============================================================================
   182	// SDF Helpers for Debug Shapes (Phase 1)
   183	// ============================================================================
   184	
   185	float SDF_Circle(float2 p, float2 center, float radius) {
   186	    return length(p - center) - radius;
   187	}
   188	
   189	float SDF_RoundedBox(float2 p, float2 center, float2 halfSize, float radius) {
   190	    float2 d = abs(p - center) - halfSize + radius;
   191	    return length(max(d, 0.0)) + min(max(d.x, d.y), 0.0) - radius;
   192	}
   193	
   194	// Calculate grid glow for a given ray direction and preset
   195	float3 CalculateGrid(float3 ray, int preset, int colorIdx) {
   196	    float numLong, numLat;
   197	    switch(preset) {
   198	        case 0: numLong = 8.0;  numLat = 5.0;  break;
   199	        case 1: numLong = 12.0; numLat = 8.0;  break;
   200	        case 2: numLong = 16.0; numLat = 10.0; break;
   201	        case 3: numLong = 24.0; numLat = 15.0; break;
   202	        default: numLong = 32.0; numLat = 20.0; break;
   203	    }
   204	    
   205	    float thetaStep = 6.2831853 / numLong;
   206	    float phiStep = 3.1415927 / numLat;
   207	    int maxMeridianIdx = int(numLong) - 1;
   208	    int maxParallelIdx = int(numLat) - 1;
   209	    
   210	    // Convert to spherical coordinates
   211	    float phi = acos(clamp(ray.y, -1.0, 1.0));
   212	    float theta = atan2(ray.z, ray.x);
   213	    
   214	    float3 gridColor = kGridColors[colorIdx];
   215	    
   216	    // --- MERIDIANS ---
   217	    float t = (theta + 3.14159265) / thetaStep - 0.5;
   218	    int cellIdx = int(floor(t));
   219	    int mLeft = cellIdx;
   220	    int mRight = cellIdx + 1;
   221	    if (mLeft < 0) mLeft = maxMeridianIdx;
   222	    if (mRight > maxMeridianIdx) mRight = 0;
   223	    
   224	    float distLeft = abs(theta - (-3.14159265 + (float(mLeft) + 0.5) * thetaStep));
   225	    distLeft = min(distLeft, 6.2831853 - distLeft);
   226	    float distRight = abs(theta - (-3.14159265 + (float(mRight) + 0.5) * thetaStep));
   227	    distRight = min(distRight, 6.2831853 - distRight);
   228	    
   229	    float surfLeft = sin(phi) * distLeft;
   230	    float surfRight = sin(phi) * distRight;
   231	    
   232	    float noiseLeft, noiseRight;
   233	    switch(preset) {
   234	        case 0:  noiseLeft = meridianNoise_Jumbo[mLeft];  noiseRight = meridianNoise_Jumbo[mRight];  break;
   235	        case 1:  noiseLeft = meridianNoise_Large[mLeft];  noiseRight = meridianNoise_Large[mRight];  break;
   236	        case 2:  noiseLeft = meridianNoise_Medium[mLeft]; noiseRight = meridianNoise_Medium[mRight]; break;
   237	        case 3:  noiseLeft = meridianNoise_Small[mLeft];  noiseRight = meridianNoise_Small[mRight];  break;
   238	        default: noiseLeft = meridianNoise_Tiny[mLeft];   noiseRight = meridianNoise_Tiny[mRight];   break;
   239	    }
   240	    
   241	    // Pole fade
   242	    float poleFadeStart = 0.5 * phiStep;
   243	    float poleFade = smoothstep(0.0, poleFadeStart, phi) * 
   244	                     smoothstep(3.1415927, 3.1415927 - poleFadeStart, phi);
   245	    
   246	    float3 glowM = gridColor * GridIntensity * (
   247	        noiseLeft / (surfLeft + GridThickness) + 
   248	        noiseRight / (surfRight + GridThickness)
   249	    ) * poleFade;
   250	    
   251	    // --- PARALLELS ---
   252	    float p = phi / phiStep - 0.5;
   253	    int pCell = int(floor(p));
   254	    int pLow = max(0, min(maxParallelIdx, pCell));
   255	    int pHigh = max(0, min(maxParallelIdx, pCell + 1));
   256	    
   257	    float distLow = abs(phi - (float(pLow) + 0.5) * phiStep);
   258	    float distHigh = abs(phi - (float(pHigh) + 0.5) * phiStep);
   259	    
   260	    float noiseLow, noiseHigh;
   261	    switch(preset) {
   262	        case 0:  noiseLow = parallelNoise_Jumbo[pLow];  noiseHigh = parallelNoise_Jumbo[pHigh];  break;
   263	        case 1:  noiseLow = parallelNoise_Large[pLow];  noiseHigh = parallelNoise_Large[pHigh];  break;
   264	        case 2:  noiseLow = parallelNoise_Medium[pLow]; noiseHigh = parallelNoise_Medium[pHigh]; break;
   265	        case 3:  noiseLow = parallelNoise_Small[pLow];  noiseHigh = parallelNoise_Small[pHigh];  break;
   266	        default: noiseLow = parallelNoise_Tiny[pLow];   noiseHigh = parallelNoise_Tiny[pHigh];   break;
   267	    }
   268	    
   269	    float3 glowP = gridColor * GridIntensity * (
   270	        noiseLow / (distLow + GridThickness) + 
   271	        noiseHigh / (distHigh + GridThickness)
   272	    );
   273	    
   274	    return glowM + glowP;
   275	}
   276	
   277	float4 PSMain(PSInput input) : SV_Target {
   278	    float2 fragCoord = input.uv * Resolution;
   279	    
   280	    float aspect = Resolution.x / Resolution.y;
   281	    float2 uv = float2(
   282	        (input.uv.x - 0.5) * 2.0 * aspect,
   283	        (input.uv.y - 0.5) * 2.0
   284	    );
   285	    
   286	    float2 perp = float2(-uv.y, uv.x) * ChromaticAberrationStrength;
   287	    
   288	    float2 uvR = uv + perp;
   289	    float2 uvG = uv;
   290	    float2 uvB = uv - perp;
   291	    
   292	    float focalLength = max(FocalLength, 0.001);
   293	    
   294	    float3 rayR = normalize(float3(uvR.x, uvR.y, focalLength));
   295	    float3 rayG = normalize(float3(uvG.x, uvG.y, focalLength));
   296	    float3 rayB = normalize(float3(uvB.x, uvB.y, focalLength));
   297	    
   298	    rayR = ViewToWorld(rayR, CameraRight, CameraUp, CameraForward);
   299	    rayG = ViewToWorld(rayG, CameraRight, CameraUp, CameraForward);
   300	    rayB = ViewToWorld(rayB, CameraRight, CameraUp, CameraForward);
   301	    
   302	    rayR = ApplyPreRotation(rayR, PreRotationYaw, PreRotationPitch);
   303	    rayG = ApplyPreRotation(rayG, PreRotationYaw, PreRotationPitch);
   304	    rayB = ApplyPreRotation(rayB, PreRotationYaw, PreRotationPitch);
   305	    
   306	    int preset = GridSizePreset;
   307	    int colorIdx = GridColorIndex;
   308	    float3 colR = CalculateGrid(rayR, preset, colorIdx);
   309	    float3 colG = CalculateGrid(rayG, preset, colorIdx);
   310	    float3 colB = CalculateGrid(rayB, preset, colorIdx);
   311	    
   312	    float3 col;
   313	    col.r = colR.r;
   314	    col.g = colG.g;
   315	    col.b = colB.b;
   316	    
   317	    // ============================================================================
   318	    // SELECTION CIRCLE + INFO BOX + TEXT
   319	    // ============================================================================
   320	    if (SelectionCircleEnabled) {
   321	        float3 shapeColor = kGridColors[GridColorIndex];
   322	        float3 shapeAccum = float3(0, 0, 0);
   323	        
   324	        float2 center = SelectionCircleCenter;
   325	        float r = SelectionCircleRadius;
   326	        float thick = SelectionCircleThickness;
   327	        float flicker = SelectionCircleT >= 1.0 ? 1.0 : SelectionCircleT;
   328	        
   329	        // --- Info box black backing (FIRST - darkens background) ---
   330	        float2 boxCenter = DebugBoxTopLeft + DebugBoxSize * 0.5;
   331	        float2 boxHalfSize = DebugBoxSize * 0.5;
   332	        float boxCornerRad = 0.005;
   333	        
   334	        float backSdf = SDF_RoundedBox(uv, boxCenter, boxHalfSize, boxCornerRad);
   335	        float backMask = smoothstep(0.0, 0.003, -backSdf);
   336	        col = lerp(col, col * 0.05, backMask);
   337	        
   338	        // --- Selection circle glow ---
   339	        float2 caOffset = perp * r * 0.1;
   340	        float dR = SDF_Circle(uvR.xy, center + caOffset, r);
   341	        float dG = SDF_Circle(uvG.xy, center, r);
   342	        float dB = SDF_Circle(uvB.xy, center - caOffset, r);
   343	        
   344	        shapeAccum += shapeColor * SelectionCircleIntensity * flicker * float3(
   345	            1.0 / (abs(dR) + thick),
   346	            1.0 / (abs(dG) + thick),
   347	            1.0 / (abs(dB) + thick)
   348	        );
   349	        
   350	        // --- Info box outline ---
   351	        float dbR = SDF_RoundedBox(uvR.xy, boxCenter + caOffset, boxHalfSize, boxCornerRad);
   352	        float dbG = SDF_RoundedBox(uvG.xy, boxCenter, boxHalfSize, boxCornerRad);
   353	        float dbB = SDF_RoundedBox(uvB.xy, boxCenter - caOffset, boxHalfSize, boxCornerRad);
   354	        
   355	        shapeAccum += shapeColor * SelectionCircleIntensity * flicker * float3(
   356	            1.0 / (abs(dbR) + DebugBoxThickness),
   357	            1.0 / (abs(dbG) + DebugBoxThickness),
   358	            1.0 / (abs(dbB) + DebugBoxThickness)
   359	        );
   360	        
   361	        // --- Text rendering (on top of darkened background) ---
   362	        // TextOrigin and TextAreaSize are in shader-uv space (center=0, +Y=UP)
   363	        // Apply chromatic aberration: sample text 3 times with RGB offset UVs
   364	        float2 textLocalR = (uvR - TextOrigin) / TextAreaSize;
   365	        float2 textLocalG = (uvG - TextOrigin) / TextAreaSize;
   366	        float2 textLocalB = (uvB - TextOrigin) / TextAreaSize;
   367	        
   368	        // No Y-flip needed - texture is rendered right-side-up by compute shader
   369	        
   370	        // Sample text coverage for each channel separately (chromatic aberration)
   371	        float coverageR = 0.0, coverageG = 0.0, coverageB = 0.0;
   372	        
   373	        if (textLocalR.x >= 0.0 && textLocalR.x <= 1.0 && 
   374	            textLocalR.y >= 0.0 && textLocalR.y <= 1.0)
   375	            coverageR = TextTexture.SampleLevel(TextSampler, textLocalR, 0).r;
   376	            
   377	        if (textLocalG.x >= 0.0 && textLocalG.x <= 1.0 && 
   378	            textLocalG.y >= 0.0 && textLocalG.y <= 1.0)
   379	            coverageG = TextTexture.SampleLevel(TextSampler, textLocalG, 0).r;
   380	            
   381	        if (textLocalB.x >= 0.0 && textLocalB.x <= 1.0 && 
   382	            textLocalB.y >= 0.0 && textLocalB.y <= 1.0)
   383	            coverageB = TextTexture.SampleLevel(TextSampler, textLocalB, 0).r;
   384	        
   385	        // Add text with per-channel coverage for chromatic aberration effect
   386	        shapeAccum += shapeColor * SelectionTextT * float3(coverageR, coverageG, coverageB);
   387	        
   388	        col += shapeAccum;
   389	    }
   390	    
   391	    float phase = frac(fragCoord.x / 3.0);
   392	    float3 phosphor;
   393	    if (phase < 0.33)       phosphor = float3(1.0, 0.3, 0.3);
   394	    else if (phase < 0.66)  phosphor = float3(0.3, 1.0, 0.3);
   395	    else                    phosphor = float3(0.3, 0.3, 1.0);
   396	    
   397	    col = col * phosphor * 1.4;
   398	    col = tanh(col);
   399	    
   400	    float2 uvNormalized = input.uv * 2.0 - 1.0;
   401	    uvNormalized.x *= aspect;
   402	    float distFromCenter = length(uvNormalized);
   403	    
   404	    float vignette = 1.0 - smoothstep(VignetteStart, VignetteEnd, distFromCenter) * VignetteStrength;
   405	    col *= vignette;
   406	    
   407	    return float4(col, 1.0);
   408	}


</details>

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

The logs show the text texture is being generated at 598x240, but it's appearing at roughly half the expected size on screen. I've been tracing the data flow to find where this scaling occurs. 

In `KartographerSelector.cs`, the C# code handles the layout and measures the text. It uses a conversion factor (`pixelsToUvY = 2.0f / Screen.height`) to translate pixel dimensions into UV space. For a 4K screen (3840x2160), this results in a UV height of approximately 0.2222 for the 240px text. When the shader uses this UV height to map the texture onto the quad, the math suggests the pixels should be 1:1.

But wait—the shader is clearly showing the text at a smaller size. Looking at `KartographerPS.hlsl`, specifically lines 364-366, the shader transforms the UV coordinates. It calculates a `textLocal` coordinate by subtracting `TextOrigin` from the current UV and then dividing by `TextAreaSize`. This is a standard 0-1 normalization for texture sampling. 

Actually, if the `TextAreaSize` passed from C# is already the scaled-down UV size (0.2222), and we divide the current UV (which is in the range -1 to 1) by that value, we are effectively creating a mapping where the texture is stretched or compressed to fit that specific UV rectangle. If the aspect ratio of `TextAreaSize` doesn't match the aspect ratio of the source text texture (598/240 = 2.49), the text will be squashed.

Unless... the issue isn't just aspect ratio, but the fundamental scale calculation. The C# side uses `Screen.height` to determine the UV size. If the shader is receiving these values but the final output is still wrong, I need to check if the native plugin is overriding or modifying these parameters. 

The C# code calls `StarfieldNative.CR_StarfieldSetKartographerParams(ref kartParams)`. This suggests there's a native-side structure that receives these values. If the native plugin is responsible for the final rasterization of the starfield and the overlay, it might be applying its own scaling logic to the `KartographerParams` before they reach the GPU, or perhaps the `TextAreaSize` is being interpreted as a different unit (like screen-space pixels vs. UV coordinates) in the C++ layer.

I need to verify how `KartographerParams` is defined and handled in the native plugin. If the C++ code is modifying the `textWidthUV` or `textHeightUV` values, or if it's applying a global scale to the overlay based on the render target size, that would explain the discrepancy. I will examine `StarfieldNative.cpp` to see the implementation of the parameter setting function and the structure of `KartographerParams`.

</details>

   288	    // Base grid params (64 bytes) - offsets 0-63
   289	    float ResolutionX;              // offset 0
   290	    float ResolutionY;              // offset 4
   291	    float Time;                     // offset 8
   292	    float GridIntensity;            // offset 12
   293	    float GridThickness;            // offset 16
   294	    float ChromaticAberrationStrength; // offset 20
   295	    float VignetteStrength;         // offset 24
   296	    float VignetteStart;            // offset 28
   297	    float VignetteEnd;              // offset 32
   298	    float PreRotationYaw;           // offset 36
   299	    float PreRotationPitch;         // offset 40
   300	    int GridSizePreset;             // offset 44
   301	    int GridColorIndex;             // offset 48
   302	    float _pad1;                    // offset 52
   303	    float _pad2;                    // offset 56
   304	    float _padAlignCamera;          // offset 60
   305	    
   306	    // Camera basis (48 bytes) - offsets 64-111
   307	    float CameraRightX;             // offset 64
   308	    float CameraRightY;             // offset 68
   309	    float CameraRightZ;             // offset 72
   310	    float _pad3;                    // offset 76
   311	    float CameraUpX;                // offset 80
   312	    float CameraUpY;                // offset 84
   313	    float CameraUpZ;                // offset 88
   314	    float _pad4;                    // offset 92
   315	    float CameraForwardX;           // offset 96
   316	    float CameraForwardY;           // offset 100
   317	    float CameraForwardZ;           // offset 104
   318	    float _pad5;                    // offset 108
   319	    
   320	    // Debug shapes (32 bytes) - offsets 112-143
   321	    int DebugShapesEnabled;         // offset 112
   322	    float _pad6;                    // offset 116
   323	    float _pad7;                    // offset 120
   324	    float _pad8;                    // offset 124
   325	    float DebugCircleCenterX;       // offset 128
   326	    float DebugCircleCenterY;       // offset 132
   327	    float DebugCircleRadius;        // offset 136
   328	    float DebugCircleThickness;     // offset 140
   329	    float DebugBoxTopLeftX;         // offset 144
   330	    float DebugBoxTopLeftY;         // offset 148
   331	    float DebugBoxSizeX;            // offset 152
   332	    float DebugBoxSizeY;            // offset 156
   333	    float DebugBoxThickness;        // offset 160
   334	    float DebugShapeIntensity;      // offset 164
   335	    float _pad9;                    // offset 168
   336	    float FocalLength;              // offset 172
   337	    
   338	    // Selection circle (32 bytes) - offsets 176-207
   339	    int SelectionCircleEnabled;     // offset 176
   340	    float _padSelection1;           // offset 180
   341	    float _padSelection2;           // offset 184
   342	    float _padSelection3;           // offset 188
   343	    float SelectionCircleCenterX;   // offset 192
   344	    float SelectionCircleCenterY;   // offset 196
   345	    float SelectionCircleT;         // offset 200
   346	    float SelectionCircleIntensity; // offset 204
   347	    float SelectionCircleThickness; // offset 208
   348	    float SelectionCircleRadius;    // offset 212
   349	    float _padSelection4;           // offset 216
   350	    float _padSelection5;           // offset 220
   351	    float _padSelection6;           // offset 224
   352	    float _padSelection7;           // offset 228
   353	    
   354	    // Text stub (16 bytes) - offsets 232-247
   355	    float TextOriginX;              // offset 232
   356	    float TextOriginY;              // offset 236
   357	    float TextAreaSizeX;            // offset 240
   358	    float TextAreaSizeY;            // offset 244
   359	    float SelectionTextT;           // offset 248
   360	    float _pad12;                   // offset 252
   361	};
   362	
   363	// Soft bloom constant buffer layouts (must match HLSL exactly)
   364	struct PrefilterParams {
   365	    float SourceSizeX, SourceSizeY;        // float4[0].xy
   366	    float InvSourceSizeX, InvSourceSizeY;  // float4[0].zw
   367	    float BloomThreshold;                  // float4[1].x
   368	    float BloomKnee;                       // float4[1].y (NEW: was BloomSpread)
   369	    float OutputSizeX, OutputSizeY;        // float4[1].zw
   370	    float InvOutputSizeX, InvOutputSizeY;  // float4[2].xy (NEW: quarter-res inverse size)
   371	    float Pad[2];                          // float4[2].zw (padding to 48 bytes)
   372	};
   373	
   374	struct BlurParams {
   375	    float TexelSizeX, TexelSizeY;
   376	    float BloomSpread;      // Match prefilter spread for consistency
   377	    float Pad;
   378	};
   379	
   380	struct SoftCompositeParams {
   381	    float ScreenSizeX, ScreenSizeY;
   382	    float InvScreenSizeX, InvScreenSizeY;
   383	    float BloomIntensity;     // Final intensity multiplier
   384	    float ExposureEV;
   385	    int EnableTonemapping;
   386	    float Pad1;
   387	    float ExtinctionZenith;
   388	    float ExtinctionHorizon;
   389	    float Pad2[2];
   390	    float AtmosphereUpX, AtmosphereUpY, AtmosphereUpZ;
   391	    float Pad3;
   392	    
   393	    // Global dimming factors (new - 16 bytes added)
   394	    float SunGlareDimming;
   395	    float PlanetaryDimming;
   396	    float GlobalDimming;
   397	    float _padFinal;  // Ensure 16-byte alignment (80 bytes total)
   398	};
   399	
   400	// ============================================================================
   401	// Utility Functions for Star Generation
   402	// ============================================================================
   403	
   404	// Calculate spectral type enum from temperature
   405	static int32_t TemperatureToSpectralType(float temp) {
   406	    if (temp < 3500.0f) return 6;      // M-type (red)
   407	    else if (temp < 4500.0f) return 5; // K-type (orange)
   408	    else if (temp < 5778.0f) return 4; // G-type (yellow)
   409	    else if (temp < 7200.0f) return 3; // F-type (yellow-white)
   410	    else if (temp < 9500.0f) return 2; // A-type (white)
   411	    else if (temp < 20000.0f) return 1; // B-type (blue-white)
   412	    else return 0;                      // O-type (blue)
   413	}
   414	
   415	// Luminosity class enum
   416	enum LuminosityClass {
   417	    LUM_SUPERGIANT = 0,  // Ia, Ib
   418	    LUM_GIANT = 1,       // II, III
   419	    LUM_SUBGIANT = 2,    // IV
   420	    LUM_DWARF = 3,       // V (Main Sequence) - 90% of stars
   421	    LUM_COUNT = 4
   422	};
   423	
   424	// Absolute Magnitude (M_v) lookup table for Main Sequence (Dwarf) stars by spectral type
   425	// Spectral types: O=0, B=1, A=2, F=3, G=4, K=5, M=6
   426	static const float AbsMag_MainSequence[7] = {
   427	    -4.0f,   // O-type (Blue) - Very luminous
   428	    -1.5f,   // B-type (Blue-white)
   429	    +0.7f,   // A-type (White) - Sirius-like
   430	    +2.5f,   // F-type (Yellow-white)
   431	    +4.8f,   // G-type (Yellow) - Sun-like
   432	    +6.5f,   // K-type (Orange)
   433	    +9.0f    // M-type (Red) - Very dim
   434	};
   435	
   436	// Absolute Magnitude for Giant stars (luminous evolved stars)
   437	static const float AbsMag_Giant[7] = {
   438	    -6.5f,   // O-type giants (rare)
   439	    -4.0f,   // B-type giants
   440	    -0.5f,   // A-type giants
   441	    +1.0f,   // F-type giants
   442	    +2.5f,   // G-type giants
   443	    +4.0f,   // K-type giants
   444	    +5.5f    // M-type giants (very luminous, e.g., Betelgeuse)
   445	};
   446	
   447	// Absolute Magnitude for Supergiant stars (extremely luminous)
   448	static const float AbsMag_Supergiant[7] = {
   449	    -7.5f,   // O-type supergiants
   450	    -6.5f,   // B-type supergiants (e.g., Rigel)
   451	    -3.0f,   // A-type supergiants
   452	    -1.0f,   // F-type supergiants
   453	    +1.0f,   // G-type supergiants
   454	    +2.5f,   // K-type supergiants (e.g., Betelgeuse)
   455	    +4.0f    // M-type supergiants (e.g., Antares)
   456	};
   457	
   458	// Assign luminosity class based on random hash and star properties
   459	// 90% Dwarfs (main sequence), 9% Giants, 1% Supergiants
   460	static LuminosityClass AssignLuminosityClass(float randomHash, int32_t spectralType, float normalizedBrightness) {
   461	    // Bright red stars are likely giants/supergiants (red giant branch)
   462	    if (spectralType >= 5 && normalizedBrightness < 0.2f) {
   463	        // 30% chance of being a giant/supergiant if bright and red
   464	        if (randomHash < 0.3f) {
   465	            return (randomHash < 0.1f) ? LUM_SUPERGIANT : LUM_GIANT;
   466	        }
   467	    }
   468	    
   469	    // Standard distribution
   470	    if (randomHash < 0.90f) return LUM_DWARF;        // 90% main sequence
   471	    else if (randomHash < 0.99f) return LUM_GIANT;   // 9% giants
   472	    else return LUM_SUPERGIANT;                       // 1% supergiants
   473	}
   474	
   475	// Get absolute magnitude based on spectral type and luminosity class
   476	static float GetAbsoluteMagnitude(int32_t spectralType, LuminosityClass lumClass) {
   477	    // Clamp spectral type to valid range
   478	    if (spectralType < 0) spectralType = 0;
   479	    if (spectralType > 6) spectralType = 6;
   480	    
   481	    switch (lumClass) {
   482	        case LUM_SUPERGIANT:
   483	            return AbsMag_Supergiant[spectralType];
   484	        case LUM_GIANT:
   485	            return AbsMag_Giant[spectralType];
   486	        case LUM_SUBGIANT:
   487	            // Subgiants are between dwarfs and giants
   488	            return (AbsMag_MainSequence[spectralType] + AbsMag_Giant[spectralType]) * 0.5f;
   489	        case LUM_DWARF:
   490	        default:
   491	            return AbsMag_MainSequence[spectralType];
   492	    }
   493	}
   494	
   495	// Calculate distance in parsecs using the Distance Modulus
   496	// d = 10^((m - M + 5) / 5)
   497	// where m = apparent magnitude, M = absolute magnitude
   498	// forcedLumClass: optional override for luminosity class (for realistic mode)
   499	static float CalculateDistancePc(float apparentMag, int32_t spectralType, float randomHash, float normalizedBrightness, LuminosityClass forcedLumClass = LUM_COUNT, int heroIndex = -1) {
   500	    // Assign luminosity class (or use forced class if provided)
   501	    LuminosityClass lumClass = (forcedLumClass != LUM_COUNT) ? forcedLumClass : AssignLuminosityClass(randomHash, spectralType, normalizedBrightness);
   502	    
   503	    
   504	    // Get absolute magnitude for this spectral type and luminosity class
   505	    float absoluteMag = GetAbsoluteMagnitude(spectralType, lumClass);
   506	    
   507	    // Add some random variation to absolute magnitude (stars aren't all identical)
   508	    // +/- 0.5 magnitude scatter
   509	    absoluteMag += (randomHash - 0.5f) * 1.0f;
   510	    
   511	    // Distance Modulus: m - M = 5 * log10(d) - 5
   512	    // Solving for d: d = 10^((m - M + 5) / 5)
   513	    float distanceModulus = apparentMag - absoluteMag + 5.0f;
   514	    float distance = powf(10.0f, distanceModulus / 5.0f);
   515	    
   516	    // Clamp to reasonable astronomical range
   517	    // Nearest stars: ~1.3 pc (Proxima), Galaxy: ~50,000 pc
   518	    return fmaxf(0.5f, fminf(50000.0f, distance));
   519	}
   520	
   521	// ============================================================================
   522	// Catalog Generation Math (Ported from HLSL)
   523	// ============================================================================
   524	
   525	inline float Frac(float x) {
   526	    return x - floorf(x);
   527	}
   528	
   529	inline float Dot(const float3& a, const float3& b) {
   530	    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
   531	}
   532	
   533	inline float Length(const float3& v) {
   534	    return sqrtf(v.x * v.x + v.y * v.y + v.z * v.z);
   535	}
   536	
   537	inline float3 Normalize(const float3& v) {
   538	    float len = Length(v);
   539	    if (len < 0.0001f) return float3(0, 0, 0);
   540	    float inv = 1.0f / len;
   541	    return float3(v.x * inv, v.y * inv, v.z * inv);
   542	}
   543	
   544	// Hash functions (must match HLSL exactly)
   545	static float3 Hash33(const float3& p) {
   546	    float3 q;
   547	    q.x = Dot(p, float3(127.1f, 311.7f, 74.7f));
   548	    q.y = Dot(p, float3(269.5f, 183.3f, 246.1f));
   549	    q.z = Dot(p, float3(113.5f, 271.9f, 124.6f));
   550	    
   551	    return float3(
   552	        Frac(sinf(q.x) * 43758.5453f),
   553	        Frac(sinf(q.y) * 43758.5453f),
   554	        Frac(sinf(q.z) * 43758.5453f)
   555	    );
   556	}
   557	
   558	static float Hash13(const float3& p) {
   559	    float q = Dot(p, float3(12.9898f, 78.233f, 45.164f));
   560	    return Frac(sinf(q) * 43758.5453f);
   561	}
   562	
   563	// Value noise for bulge (simplified fbm)
   564	static float ValueNoise(const float3& p) {
   565	    float3 i = float3(floorf(p.x), floorf(p.y), floorf(p.z));
   566	    float3 f = float3(Frac(p.x), Frac(p.y), Frac(p.z));
   567	    f.x = f.x * f.x * (3.0f - 2.0f * f.x);
   568	    f.y = f.y * f.y * (3.0f - 2.0f * f.y);
   569	    f.z = f.z * f.z * (3.0f - 2.0f * f.z);
   570	    
   571	    // Simplified - just return hash of integer coords for now
   572	    return Hash13(i);
   573	}
   574	
   575	// FBM (Fractal Brownian Motion) for organic hierarchical detail
   576	// octaves: number of noise layers (4 recommended for bulge, 3 for clustering)
   577	// lacunarity: frequency multiplier per octave (typically 2.0)
   578	// gain: amplitude multiplier per octave (typically 0.5)
   579	static float FBM(const float3& p, int octaves, float lacunarity, float gain) {
   580	    float value = 0.0f;
   581	    float amplitude = 0.5f;
   582	    float frequency = 1.0f;
   583	    
   584	    for(int i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
   585	        value += amplitude * ValueNoise(float3(p.x * frequency, p.y * frequency, p.z * frequency));
   586	        amplitude *= gain;
   587	        frequency *= lacunarity;
   588	    }
   589	    
   590	    return value;
   591	}
   592	
   593	// Galactic density calculation (matches HLSL get_galactic_density)
   594	static float GetGalacticDensityCPU(const float3& rayDir, 
   595	    float flatness, float falloff, float bandBoost, float bandSharpness,
   596	    const float3& planeNormal, float bulgeIntensity, const float3& bulgeCenter,
   597	    float bulgeWidth, float bulgeHeight, float bulgeSoftness, 
   598	    float bulgeNoiseScale, float bulgeNoiseStr) 
   599	{
   600	    if (flatness <= 0.001f) return 1.0f;
   601	    
   602	    float3 n = Normalize(planeNormal);
   603	    float sinLatitude = Dot(rayDir, n);
   604	    float absSinLat = fabsf(sinLatitude);
   605	    float cosLatitude = sqrtf(max(0.0f, 1.0f - sinLatitude * sinLatitude));
   606	    
   607	    float exponent = falloff * flatness;
   608	    float baseDensity = powf(max(cosLatitude, 0.0f), exponent);
   609	    float coreDensity = bandBoost * powf(max(cosLatitude, 0.0f), bandSharpness);
   610	    
   611	    float bulgeDensity = 0.0f;
   612	    if (bulgeIntensity > 0.0f) {
   613	        float3 projectedRay = float3(
   614	            rayDir.x - sinLatitude * n.x,
   615	            rayDir.y - sinLatitude * n.y,
   616	            rayDir.z - sinLatitude * n.z
   617	        );
   618	        float3 centerDir = Normalize(bulgeCenter);
   619	        float3 projectedCenter = float3(
   620	            centerDir.x - Dot(centerDir, n) * n.x,
   621	            centerDir.y - Dot(centerDir, n) * n.y,
   622	            centerDir.z - Dot(centerDir, n) * n.z
   623	        );
   624	        
   625	        float centerLen = Length(projectedCenter);
   626	        if (centerLen > 0.001f) {
   627	            float3 normProjCenter = float3(
   628	                projectedCenter.x / centerLen,
   629	                projectedCenter.y / centerLen,
   630	                projectedCenter.z / centerLen
   631	            );
   632	            float3 normProjRay = Normalize(projectedRay);
   633	            
   634	            float cosLong = Dot(normProjRay, normProjCenter);
   635	            float longDist = 1.0f - cosLong;
   636	            float latDist = absSinLat;
   637	            
   638	            float dx = longDist / bulgeWidth;
   639	            float dy = latDist / bulgeHeight;
   640	            float t = sqrtf(dx*dx + dy*dy);
   641	            
   642	            float softnessCurve = powf(max(bulgeSoftness, 0.0f), 0.1f);
   643	            float edgeExponent = 20.0f * (1.0f - softnessCurve) + 0.1f * softnessCurve;
   644	            float baseFalloff = powf(max(0.0f, 1.0f - t), edgeExponent);
   645	            
   646	            // FBM for organic bulge edge breakup with hierarchical detail
   647	            // 4 octaves gives big structural variation with fine detail
   648	            float3 noisePos = float3(rayDir.x * bulgeNoiseScale * 0.1f, 
   649	                                     rayDir.y * bulgeNoiseScale * 0.1f, 
   650	                                     rayDir.z * bulgeNoiseScale * 0.1f);
   651	            float noise = FBM(noisePos, 4, 2.0f, 0.5f);
   652	            
   653	            // Secondary detail layer for "tighter" breakup at smaller scales
   654	            float3 detailPos = float3(noisePos.x * 2.0f, noisePos.y * 2.0f, noisePos.z * 2.0f);
   655	            float detailNoise = FBM(detailPos, 3, 2.0f, 0.5f) * 0.5f;
   656	            float combinedNoise = (noise + detailNoise) * 0.6667f;
   657	            
   658	            float densityMod = 1.0f - (combinedNoise * bulgeNoiseStr);
   659	            float falloffBulge = baseFalloff * densityMod;
   660	            
   661	            bulgeDensity = bulgeIntensity * falloffBulge;
   662	        }
   663	    }
   664	    
   665	    return baseDensity + coreDensity + bulgeDensity;
   666	}
   667	
   668	// Blackbody color calculation (Tanner Helland algorithm)
   669	// Returns RGB in range [0, 1] for given temperature in Kelvin
   670	static float3 BlackbodyRGB(float temperature)
   671	{
   672	    float t = fmaxf(1000.0f, fminf(40000.0f, temperature));
   673	    float tmp = t / 100.0f;
   674	    
   675	    float r, g, b;
   676	    
   677	    // Red
   678	    if (tmp <= 66.0f) {
   679	        r = 255.0f;
   680	    } else {
   681	        r = 329.698727446f * powf(tmp - 60.0f, -0.1332047592f);
   682	        r = fmaxf(0.0f, fminf(255.0f, r));
   683	    }
   684	    
   685	    // Green
   686	    if (tmp <= 66.0f) {
   687	        g = 99.4708025861f * logf(tmp) - 161.1195681661f;
   688	        g = fmaxf(0.0f, fminf(255.0f, g));
   689	    } else {
   690	        g = 288.1221695283f * powf(tmp - 60.0f, -0.0755148492f);
   691	        g = fmaxf(0.0f, fminf(255.0f, g));
   692	    }
   693	    
   694	    // Blue
   695	    if (tmp >= 66.0f) {
   696	        b = 255.0f;
   697	    } else if (tmp <= 19.0f) {
   698	        b = 0.0f;
   699	    } else {
   700	        b = 138.5177312231f * logf(tmp - 10.0f) - 305.0447927307f;
   701	        b = fmaxf(0.0f, fminf(255.0f, b));
   702	    }
   703	    
   704	    return float3(r / 255.0f, g / 255.0f, b / 255.0f);
   705	}
   706	
   707	// Apply saturation to color: 0.5=realistic, 1.0=natural, 4.0=hyper-vivid (max for this star)
   708	// catalogID provides per-star variation in saturation rate
   709	static float3 ApplySaturation(float3 baseColor, float sliderValue, int32_t catalogID)
   710	{
   711	    // Per-star variation hash (0-1)
   712	    float variationHash = Hash13(float3((float)catalogID * 12.9898f, (float)catalogID * 78.233f, (float)catalogID * 45.164f));
   713	    
   714	    // Calculate max saturation this specific star can reach before clipping to black in any channel
   715	    // From: 1 + (c - 1) * t = 0  →  t = 1 / (1 - c) for any channel where c < 1
   716	    float maxT = 100.0f;
   717	    if (baseColor.x < 0.999f) maxT = fminf(maxT, 1.0f / (1.0f - baseColor.x));
   718	    if (baseColor.y < 0.999f) maxT = fminf(maxT, 1.0f / (1.0f - baseColor.y));
   719	    if (baseColor.z < 0.999f) maxT = fminf(maxT, 1.0f / (1.0f - baseColor.z));
   720	    
   721	    // Clamp maxT to reasonable bounds (prevents division issues, caps extreme extrapolation)
   722	    maxT = fminf(maxT, 10.0f);
   723	    
   724	    // Add per-star variation to the max (0.85x to 1.15x so some stars max out earlier/later)
   725	    maxT *= (0.85f + variationHash * 0.3f);
   726	    
   727	    // Overshoot: Push 50% past first-channel clip so red stars crush green channel
   728	    // to reach hard red (1,0,0) at slider 4.0 without affecting blue star behavior
   729	    maxT *= 1.5f;
   730	    
   731	    // Map slider [0.5, 4.0] to saturation factor t
   732	    // 0.5 -> 0.5 (white), 1.0 -> 1.0 (original), 4.0 -> maxT (fully saturated for this star)
   733	    float t;
   734	    if (sliderValue <= 1.0f) {
   735	        // Linear from white (t=0) to natural (t=1) 
   736	        // Actually we want 0.5 to give 0.5, 1.0 to give 1.0
   737	        t = sliderValue;
   738	    } else {
   739	        // Map [1.0, 4.0] to [1.0, maxT] with smooth curve
   740	        float normalized = (sliderValue - 1.0f) / 3.0f; // 0 to 1 as slider goes 1->4
   741	        // Smooth step for natural feel
   742	        normalized = normalized * normalized * (3.0f - 2.0f * normalized);
   743	        t = 1.0f + normalized * (maxT - 1.0f);
   744	    }
   745	    
   746	    // Calculate color: move away from white by factor t
   747	    float r = 1.0f + (baseColor.x - 1.0f) * t;
   748	    float g = 1.0f + (baseColor.y - 1.0f) * t;
   749	    float b = 1.0f + (baseColor.z - 1.0f) * t;
   750	    
   751	    // Clamp to valid range (safety)
   752	    r = fmaxf(0.0f, fminf(1.0f, r));
   753	    g = fmaxf(0.0f, fminf(1.0f, g));
   754	    b = fmaxf(0.0f, fminf(1.0f, b));
   755	    
   756	    return float3(r, g, b);
   757	}
   758	
   759	// Starfield Internal Functions
   760	static void EnsureStarfieldResources(ID3D11Device* device, int width, int height)
   761	{
   762	    // Check dimensions AND format (TUFX HDR toggle changes format)
   763	    if (g_StarfieldState.initialized && 
   764	        g_StarfieldState.width == width && 
   765	        g_StarfieldState.height == height &&

  1402	static void ExecuteSoftBloomRender(ID3D11DeviceContext* context, ID3D11RenderTargetView* finalRTV)
  1403	{
  1404	    if (!context || !finalRTV) return;
  1405	    
  1406	    ID3D11Device* device = nullptr;
  1407	    context->GetDevice(&device);
  1408	    if (!device) return;
  1409	    
  1410	    // Validate resources
  1411	    if (!g_StarfieldState.bloomTexture || !g_StarfieldState.bloomTempTexture || 
  1412	        !g_StarfieldState.bloomHalfTexture || !g_StarfieldState.prefilterPS || 
  1413	        !g_StarfieldState.blurXPS || !g_StarfieldState.blurPS || 
  1414	        !g_StarfieldState.softCompositePS || !g_StarfieldState.upscalePS) {
  1415	        device->Release();
  1416	        return;
  1417	    }
  1418	    
  1419	    // Disable depth testing for all bloom passes
  1420	    context->OMSetDepthStencilState(g_StarfieldState.depthState, 0);
  1421	    
  1422	    // Half-res dimensions (matching texture creation)
  1423	    int bloomWidth = g_StarfieldState.width / 2;
  1424	    int bloomHeight = g_StarfieldState.height / 2;
  1425	    if (bloomWidth < 1) bloomWidth = 1;
  1426	    if (bloomHeight < 1) bloomHeight = 1;
  1427	    
  1428	    float clearColor[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0};
  1429	    
  1430	    // ========================================================================
  1431	    // PASS 1: Prefilter + Downsample (Full-res HDR -> bloomTempTexture)
  1432	    // Rendering to half-res
  1433	    // ========================================================================
  1434	    D3D11_VIEWPORT halfResVP = {};
  1435	    halfResVP.Width = (float)bloomWidth;      // width/2
  1436	    halfResVP.Height = (float)bloomHeight;    // height/2
  1437	    halfResVP.MinDepth = 0.0f;
  1438	    halfResVP.MaxDepth = 1.0f;
  1439	    context->RSSetViewports(1, &halfResVP);
  1440	    
  1441	    context->OMSetRenderTargets(1, &g_StarfieldState.bloomTempRTV, nullptr);
  1442	    context->OMSetBlendState(nullptr, nullptr, 0xFFFFFFFF); // Disable blend
  1443	    context->ClearRenderTargetView(g_StarfieldState.bloomTempRTV, clearColor);
  1444	    
  1445	    // Update Prefilter CB
  1446	    D3D11_MAPPED_SUBRESOURCE mapped;
  1447	    if (SUCCEEDED(context->Map(g_StarfieldState.prefilterCB, 0, D3D11_MAP_WRITE_DISCARD, 0, &mapped))) {
  1448	        float* data = (float*)mapped.pData;
  1449	        data[0] = (float)g_StarfieldState.width;       // SourceSizeX (full)
  1450	        data[1] = (float)g_StarfieldState.height;      // SourceSizeY (full)
  1451	        data[2] = 1.0f / g_StarfieldState.width;       // InvSourceSizeX
  1452	        data[3] = 1.0f / g_StarfieldState.height;      // InvSourceSizeY
  1453	        data[4] = g_StarfieldState.bloomThreshold;     // BloomThreshold
  1454	        data[5] = 0.65f;                                // BloomKnee
  1455	        data[6] = (float)bloomWidth;                   // OutputSizeX - NOW width/2 (half-res)
  1456	        data[7] = (float)bloomHeight;                  // OutputSizeY - NOW height/2 (half-res)
  1457	        data[8] = 1.0f / bloomWidth;                   // InvOutputSizeX - NOW 2/width
  1458	        data[9] = 1.0f / bloomHeight;                  // InvOutputSizeY - NOW 2/height
  1459	        // Padding to fill 48 bytes
  1460	        context->Unmap(g_StarfieldState.prefilterCB, 0);
  1461	    }

  1509	    context->IASetIndexBuffer(nullptr, DXGI_FORMAT_UNKNOWN, 0);
  1510	    context->RSSetState(g_StarfieldState.rasterState);

  1512	    context->Draw(3, 0);
  1513	    context->PSSetShaderResources(0, 1, nullSRV);
  1514

  1516	    // PASS 3: Vertical Blur (bloomTexture -> bloomTempTexture)

  1518	    // ========================================================================
  1519	    context->OMSetRenderTargets(1, &g_StarfieldState.bloomTempRTV, nullptr);
  1520	    context->OMSetBlendState(nullptr, nullptr, 0xFFFFFFFF);
  1521	    
  1522	    // Same CB values (TexelSize and Spread identical for symmetric blur)
  1523	    context->PSSetShader(g_StarfieldState.blurPS, nullptr, 0); // Vertical blur
  1524	    context->PSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, &g_StarfieldState.blurCB);
  1525	    ID3D11ShaderResourceView* vertSRV[1] = {g_StarfieldState.bloomSRV};
  1526	    context->PSSetShaderResources(0, 1, vertSRV);

  1529	    context->IASetInputLayout(nullptr);
