  1539	    // Changed from 1/4 -> 1/2 to 1/2 -> Full
  1540	    // ========================================================================
  1541	    D3D11_VIEWPORT fullResVP = {};
  1542	    fullResVP.Width = (float)g_StarfieldState.width;   // Full width
  1543	    fullResVP.Height = (float)g_StarfieldState.height; // Full height
  1544	    fullResVP.MinDepth = 0.0f;
  1545	    fullResVP.MaxDepth = 1.0f;
  1546	    context->RSSetViewports(1, &fullResVP);
  1547	    
  1548	    context->OMSetRenderTargets(1, &g_StarfieldState.bloomHalfRTV, nullptr);
  1549	    context->OMSetBlendState(nullptr, nullptr, 0xFFFFFFFF);
  1550	    context->ClearRenderTargetView(g_StarfieldState.bloomHalfRTV, clearColor);
  1551	    
  1552	    context->PSSetShader(g_StarfieldState.upscalePS, nullptr, 0);
  1553	    ID3D11ShaderResourceView* upscaleSRV[1] = {g_StarfieldState.bloomTempSRV};
  1554	    context->PSSetShaderResources(0, 1, upscaleSRV);
  1555	    
  1556	    context->IASetPrimitiveTopology(D3D11_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_TRIANGLELIST);
  1557	    context->IASetInputLayout(nullptr);
  1558	    context->IASetVertexBuffers(0, 0, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr);
  1559	    context->IASetIndexBuffer(nullptr, DXGI_FORMAT_UNKNOWN, 0);
  1560	    context->RSSetState(g_StarfieldState.rasterState);
  1561	    
  1562	    context->Draw(3, 0);
  1563	    context->PSSetShaderResources(0, 1, nullSRV);
  1564	    
  1565	    // ========================================================================
  1566	    // PASS 4: Composite (Full-res stars + bloomHalf -> finalRTV)
  1567	    // ========================================================================
  1568	    D3D11_VIEWPORT fullVP = {};
  1569	    fullVP.Width = (float)g_StarfieldState.width;
  1570	    fullVP.Height = (float)g_StarfieldState.height;
  1571	    fullVP.MinDepth = 0.0f;
  1572	    fullVP.MaxDepth = 1.0f;
  1573	    context->RSSetViewports(1, &fullVP);
  1574	    
  1575	    context->OMSetRenderTargets(1, &finalRTV, nullptr);
  1576	    context->OMSetBlendState(g_StarfieldState.blendState, nullptr, 0xFFFFFFFF); // Restore blend for stars
  1577	    
  1578	    // Update Composite CB
  1579	    if (SUCCEEDED(context->Map(g_StarfieldState.compositeCB, 0, D3D11_MAP_WRITE_DISCARD, 0, &mapped))) {
  1580	        SoftCompositeParams* params = (SoftCompositeParams*)mapped.pData;
  1581	        params->ScreenSizeX = (float)g_StarfieldState.width;
  1582	        params->ScreenSizeY = (float)g_StarfieldState.height;
  1583	        params->InvScreenSizeX = 1.0f / g_StarfieldState.width;
  1584	        params->InvScreenSizeY = 1.0f / g_StarfieldState.height;
  1585	        params->BloomIntensity = g_StarfieldState.bloomIntensity;
  1586	        params->ExposureEV = g_StarfieldState.exposure;
  1587	        params->EnableTonemapping = 1;
  1588	        params->Pad1 = 0.0f;
  1589	        params->ExtinctionZenith = g_StarfieldState.extinctionZenith;
  1590	        params->ExtinctionHorizon = g_StarfieldState.extinctionHorizon;
  1591	        params->Pad2[0] = params->Pad2[1] = 0.0f;
  1592	        params->AtmosphereUpX = g_StarfieldState.atmosphereUp.x;
  1593	        params->AtmosphereUpY = g_StarfieldState.atmosphereUp.y;
  1594	        params->AtmosphereUpZ = g_StarfieldState.atmosphereUp.z;
  1595	        params->Pad3 = 0.0f;
  1596	        
  1597	        // Global scene dimming (new)
  1598	        params->SunGlareDimming = g_StarfieldState.sunGlareDimming;
  1599	        params->PlanetaryDimming = g_StarfieldState.planetaryDimming;
  1600	        params->GlobalDimming = g_StarfieldState.globalDimming;
  1601	        params->_padFinal = 0.0f;
  1602	        
  1603	        context->Unmap(g_StarfieldState.compositeCB, 0);
  1604	    }
  1605	    
  1606	    context->VSSetShader(g_StarfieldState.pass2VS, nullptr, 0);
  1607	    context->PSSetShader(g_StarfieldState.softCompositePS, nullptr, 0);
  1608	    context->PSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, &g_StarfieldState.compositeCB);
  1609	    context->PSSetSamplers(0, 1, &g_StarfieldState.linearSampler);
  1610	    
  1611	    // Composite reads full-res HDR (t0) and final bloom from bloomHalfSRV (t1, half-res)
  1612	    ID3D11ShaderResourceView* compositeSRVs[2] = {g_StarfieldState.hdrSRV, g_StarfieldState.bloomHalfSRV};
  1613	    context->PSSetShaderResources(0, 2, compositeSRVs);
  1614	    
  1615	    // Restore IA state for fullscreen triangle (matches Classic path)
  1616	    context->IASetPrimitiveTopology(D3D11_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_TRIANGLELIST);
  1617	    context->IASetInputLayout(nullptr);
  1618	    context->IASetVertexBuffers(0, 0, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr);
  1619	    context->IASetIndexBuffer(nullptr, DXGI_FORMAT_UNKNOWN, 0);
  1620	    context->RSSetState(g_StarfieldState.rasterState);
  1621	    
  1622	    context->Draw(3, 0);
  1623	    
  1624	    // Kartographer overlay pass (if enabled)
  1625	    if (g_StarfieldState.kartographerEnabled && g_StarfieldState.kartographerVS && g_StarfieldState.kartographerPS) {
  1626	        // Save current state
  1627	        ID3D11BlendState* oldBlend = nullptr;
  1628	        float oldBlendFactor[4];
  1629	        UINT oldSampleMask;
  1630	        context->OMGetBlendState(&oldBlend, oldBlendFactor, &oldSampleMask);
  1631	        
  1632	        // Set additive blend for grid overlay
  1633	        context->OMSetBlendState(g_StarfieldState.kartographerBlendState, nullptr, 0xFFFFFFFF);
  1634	        
  1635	        // Update and set Kartographer constant buffer
  1636	        MapKartographerConstantBuffer(context);
  1637	        context->PSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, &g_StarfieldState.kartographerCB);
  1638	        
  1639	        // Set Kartographer shaders
  1640	        context->VSSetShader(g_StarfieldState.kartographerVS, nullptr, 0);
  1641	        context->PSSetShader(g_StarfieldState.kartographerPS, nullptr, 0);
  1642	        
  1643	        // Bind text texture to slot t2 if available
  1644	        if (g_StarfieldState.textTextureSRV) {
  1645	            context->PSSetShaderResources(2, 1, &g_StarfieldState.textTextureSRV);
  1646	        }
  1647	        
  1648	        // Draw fullscreen triangle
  1649	        context->Draw(3, 0);
  1650	        
  1651	        // Restore blend state
  1652	        context->OMSetBlendState(oldBlend, oldBlendFactor, oldSampleMask);
  1653	        if (oldBlend) oldBlend->Release();
  1654	    }
  1655	    
  1656	    // Cleanup
  1657	    context->PSSetShaderResources(0, 2, nullSRV);
  1658	    ID3D11RenderTargetView* nullRTV = nullptr;
  1659	    context->OMSetRenderTargets(1, &nullRTV, nullptr);
  1660	}
  1661	
  1662	static void UNITY_INTERFACE_API OnStarfieldRenderEvent(int eventId)
  1663	{
  1664	    if (!g_StarfieldState.device) return;
  1665	    
  1666	    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_StarfieldState.stateMutex);
  1667	    
  1668	    ID3D11DeviceContext* context = nullptr;
  1669	    g_StarfieldState.device->GetImmediateContext(&context);
  1670	    if (!context) return;
  1671	    
  1672	    ExecuteStarfieldRender(context);
  1673	    
  1674	    // Render any registered compositor layers on top of the starfield
  1675	    if (GalaxyCamCompositor_HasLayers()) {
  1676	        ID3D11RenderTargetView* currentRTV = nullptr;
  1677	        ID3D11DepthStencilView* currentDSV = nullptr;
  1678	        context->OMGetRenderTargets(1, &currentRTV, &currentDSV);
  1679	        
  1680	        if (currentRTV) {
  1681	            GalaxyCamCompositor_RenderLayers(context, currentRTV, g_StarfieldState.width, g_StarfieldState.height);
  1682	            currentRTV->Release();
  1683	        }
  1684	        if (currentDSV) currentDSV->Release();
  1685	    }
  1686	    
  1687	    context->Release();
  1688	    
  1689	    // Increment temporal frame index
  1690	    g_StarfieldState.frameIndex = (g_StarfieldState.frameIndex + 1) & 7;
  1691	}
  1692	
  1693	extern "C" __declspec(dllexport)
  1694	void CR_StarfieldSetDimming(float sunGlareDimming, float planetaryDimming)
  1695	{
  1696	    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_StarfieldState.stateMutex);
  1697	    
  1698	    g_StarfieldState.sunGlareDimming = sunGlareDimming;
  1699	    g_StarfieldState.planetaryDimming = planetaryDimming;
  1700	    // Use whichever dims more (the darker/minimum value)
  1701	    g_StarfieldState.globalDimming = (sunGlareDimming < planetaryDimming) ? sunGlareDimming : planetaryDimming;
  1702	    
  1703	    // Safety clamp - never allow complete blackness from dimming alone
  1704	    if (g_StarfieldState.globalDimming < 0.05f)
  1705	        g_StarfieldState.globalDimming = 0.05f;
  1706	}
  1707	
  1708	extern "C" __declspec(dllexport)
  1709	void CR_StarfieldSetKartographerEnabled(unsigned char enabled)
  1710	{
  1711	    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_StarfieldState.stateMutex);
  1712	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerEnabled = (enabled != 0);
  1713	    LogToFile("[Starfield] Kartographer %s", g_StarfieldState.kartographerEnabled ? "enabled" : "disabled");
  1714	}
  1715	
  1716	static void MapKartographerConstantBuffer(ID3D11DeviceContext* context)
  1717	{
  1718	    D3D11_MAPPED_SUBRESOURCE mappedKart;
  1719	    if (SUCCEEDED(context->Map(g_StarfieldState.kartographerCB, 0, D3D11_MAP_WRITE_DISCARD, 0, &mappedKart))) {
  1720	        KartographerParams* params = (KartographerParams*)mappedKart.pData;
  1721	        
  1722	        // Base grid params
  1723	        params->ResolutionX = (float)g_StarfieldState.width;
  1724	        params->ResolutionY = (float)g_StarfieldState.height;
  1725	        params->Time = (float)g_StarfieldState.frameIndex * 0.016f;
  1726	        params->GridIntensity = g_StarfieldState.kartographerGridIntensity;
  1727	        params->GridThickness = g_StarfieldState.kartographerGridThickness;
  1728	        params->ChromaticAberrationStrength = g_StarfieldState.kartographerCAStrength;
  1729	        params->VignetteStrength = g_StarfieldState.kartographerVignetteStrength;
  1730	        params->VignetteStart = g_StarfieldState.kartographerVignetteStart;
  1731	        params->VignetteEnd = g_StarfieldState.kartographerVignetteEnd;
  1732	        params->PreRotationYaw = g_StarfieldState.kartographerPreRotationYaw;
  1733	        params->PreRotationPitch = g_StarfieldState.kartographerPreRotationPitch;
  1734	        params->GridSizePreset = g_StarfieldState.kartographerGridSizePreset;
  1735	        params->GridColorIndex = g_StarfieldState.kartographerGridColor;
  1736	        params->_pad1 = 0.0f;
  1737	        params->_pad2 = 0.0f;
  1738	        params->_padAlignCamera = 0.0f;
  1739	        
  1740	        // Camera basis
  1741	        params->CameraRightX = g_StarfieldState.cameraRight.x;
  1742	        params->CameraRightY = g_StarfieldState.cameraRight.y;
  1743	        params->CameraRightZ = g_StarfieldState.cameraRight.z;
  1744	        params->_pad3 = 0.0f;
  1745	        params->CameraUpX = g_StarfieldState.cameraUp.x;
  1746	        params->CameraUpY = g_StarfieldState.cameraUp.y;
  1747	        params->CameraUpZ = g_StarfieldState.cameraUp.z;
  1748	        params->_pad4 = 0.0f;
  1749	        params->CameraForwardX = g_StarfieldState.cameraForward.x;
  1750	        params->CameraForwardY = g_StarfieldState.cameraForward.y;
  1751	        params->CameraForwardZ = g_StarfieldState.cameraForward.z;
  1752	        params->_pad5 = 0.0f;
  1753	        
  1754	        // Debug shapes - hard-coded test values (independent of grid settings)
  1755	        params->DebugShapesEnabled = g_StarfieldState.kartographerDebugShapesEnabled;
  1756	        params->_pad6 = 0.0f;
  1757	        params->_pad7 = 0.0f;
  1758	        params->_pad8 = 0.0f;
  1759	        params->DebugCircleCenterX = 0.0f;  // Screen center in shader-uv space
  1760	        params->DebugCircleCenterY = 0.0f;
  1761	        params->DebugCircleRadius = 0.05f;
  1762	        params->DebugCircleThickness = 0.001f;
  1763	        params->DebugBoxTopLeftX = g_StarfieldState.kartographerDebugBoxTopLeftX;
  1764	        params->DebugBoxTopLeftY = g_StarfieldState.kartographerDebugBoxTopLeftY;
  1765	        params->DebugBoxSizeX = g_StarfieldState.kartographerDebugBoxSizeX;
  1766	        params->DebugBoxSizeY = g_StarfieldState.kartographerDebugBoxSizeY;
  1767	        params->DebugBoxThickness = g_StarfieldState.kartographerDebugBoxThickness;
  1768	        params->DebugShapeIntensity = 0.002f;
  1769	        params->_pad9 = 0.0f;
  1770	        params->FocalLength = g_StarfieldState.kartographerFocalLength > 0.001f ? g_StarfieldState.kartographerFocalLength : 1.732f;
  1771	        
  1772	        // Selection circle (filled from cached state set by CR_StarfieldSetKartographerParams)
  1773	        params->SelectionCircleEnabled = g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleEnabled;
  1774	        params->_padSelection1 = 0.0f;
  1775	        params->_padSelection2 = 0.0f;
  1776	        params->_padSelection3 = 0.0f;
  1777	        params->SelectionCircleCenterX = g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleCenterX;
  1778	        params->SelectionCircleCenterY = g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleCenterY;
  1779	        params->SelectionCircleT = g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleT;
  1780	        params->SelectionCircleIntensity = g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleIntensity;
  1781	        params->SelectionCircleThickness = g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleThickness;
  1782	        params->SelectionCircleRadius = g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleRadius;
  1783	        params->_padSelection4 = 0.0f;
  1784	        params->_padSelection5 = 0.0f;
  1785	        params->_padSelection6 = 0.0f;
  1786	        
  1787	        // Text params
  1788	        params->TextOriginX = g_StarfieldState.kartographerTextOriginX;
  1789	        params->TextOriginY = g_StarfieldState.kartographerTextOriginY;
  1790	        params->TextAreaSizeX = g_StarfieldState.kartographerTextAreaSizeX;
  1791	        params->TextAreaSizeY = g_StarfieldState.kartographerTextAreaSizeY;
  1792	        params->SelectionTextT = g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionTextT;
  1793	        params->_pad12 = 0.0f;
  1794	        
  1795	        context->Unmap(g_StarfieldState.kartographerCB, 0);
  1796	    }
  1797	}
  1798	
  1799	void CR_StarfieldSetKartographerParams(const KartographerParamsNative* params)
  1800	{
  1801	    if (!params) return;
  1802	    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_StarfieldState.stateMutex);
  1803	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerGridIntensity = params->GridIntensity;
  1804	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerGridThickness = params->GridThickness;
  1805	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerCAStrength = params->ChromaticAberrationStrength;
  1806	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerVignetteStrength = params->VignetteStrength;
  1807	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerVignetteStart = params->VignetteStart;
  1808	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerVignetteEnd = params->VignetteEnd;
  1809	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerPreRotationYaw = params->PreRotationYaw;
  1810	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerPreRotationPitch = params->PreRotationPitch;
  1811	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerGridSizePreset = params->GridSizePreset;
  1812	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerGridColor = params->GridColorIndex;
  1813	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerDebugShapesEnabled = params->DebugShapesEnabled;
  1814	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerFocalLength = params->FocalLength;
  1815	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerDebugBoxTopLeftX = params->DebugBoxTopLeftX;
  1816	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerDebugBoxTopLeftY = params->DebugBoxTopLeftY;
  1817	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerDebugBoxSizeX = params->DebugBoxSizeX;
  1818	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerDebugBoxSizeY = params->DebugBoxSizeY;
  1819	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerDebugBoxThickness = params->DebugBoxThickness;
  1820	    
  1821	    // Selection circle parameters (cached for CB update)
  1822	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleEnabled = params->SelectionCircleEnabled;
  1823	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleCenterX = params->SelectionCircleCenterX;
  1824	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleCenterY = params->SelectionCircleCenterY;
  1825	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleT = params->SelectionCircleT;
  1826	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleIntensity = params->SelectionCircleIntensity;
  1827	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleThickness = params->SelectionCircleThickness;
  1828	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionCircleRadius = params->SelectionCircleRadius;
  1829	    
  1830	    // Text parameters (cached for CB update)
  1831	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerTextOriginX = params->TextOriginX;
  1832	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerTextOriginY = params->TextOriginY;
  1833	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerTextAreaSizeX = params->TextAreaSizeX;
  1834	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerTextAreaSizeY = params->TextAreaSizeY;
  1835	    g_StarfieldState.kartographerSelectionTextT = params->SelectionTextT;
  1836	}
  1837	
  1838	// ============================================================================
  1839	// Text Rendering System Exports (Phase 4)
  1840	// ============================================================================
  1841	
  1842	struct TextParams {
  1843	    int GlyphCount;
  1844	    float OutputSizeX;
  1845	    float OutputSizeY;
  1846	    float Pad;
  1847	};
  1848	
  1849	extern "C" __declspec(dllexport)
  1850	void CR_TextDispatch(
  1851	    void* textSystem,
  1852	    ID3D11Texture2D* outputTexture,
  1853	    int glyphCount,
  1854	    int outputWidth,
  1855	    int outputHeight)
  1856	{
  1857	    LogToFile("[Text] CR_TextDispatch called: textSystem=%p, outputTexture=%p, glyphs=%d, size=%dx%d",
  1858	              textSystem, outputTexture, glyphCount, outputWidth, outputHeight);
  1859	    
  1860	    if (!textSystem || !outputTexture) {
  1861	        LogToFile("[Text] Early exit: textSystem=%p, outputTexture=%p", textSystem, outputTexture);
  1862	        return;
  1863	    }
  1864	    
  1865	    CinematicShaders::TextSystem* ts = static_cast<CinematicShaders::TextSystem*>(textSystem);
  1866	    LogToFile("[Text] TextSystem cast successful");
  1867	    
  1868	    // Ensure glyph buffer is created and populated
  1869	    LogToFile("[Text] Getting glyph buffer...");
  1870	    ID3D11Buffer* glyphBuffer = ts->GetOrCreateGlyphBuffer();
  1871	    if (!glyphBuffer) {
  1872	        LogToFile("[Text] FAILED: GetOrCreateGlyphBuffer returned null");
  1873	        return;
  1874	    }
  1875	    LogToFile("[Text] Glyph buffer obtained: %p", glyphBuffer);
  1876	    
  1877	    ID3D11ShaderResourceView* glyphSRV = ts->GetGlyphBufferSRV();
  1878	    if (!glyphSRV) {
  1879	        LogToFile("[Text] FAILED: GetGlyphBufferSRV returned null");
  1880	        return;
  1881	    }
  1882	    LogToFile("[Text] Glyph SRV obtained: %p", glyphSRV);
  1883	    
  1884	    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_StarfieldState.stateMutex);
  1885	    LogToFile("[Text] Got state mutex");
  1886	    
  1887	    if (!g_StarfieldState.device) {
  1888	        LogToFile("[Text] FAILED: No device available");
  1889	        return;
  1890	    }
  1891	    LogToFile("[Text] Device available: %p", g_StarfieldState.device);
  1892	    
  1893	    ID3D11DeviceContext* context = nullptr;
  1894	    g_StarfieldState.device->GetImmediateContext(&context);
  1895	    if (!context) {
  1896	        LogToFile("[Text] FAILED: GetImmediateContext returned null");
  1897	        return;
  1898	    }
  1899	    LogToFile("[Text] Context obtained: %p", context);
  1900	    
  1901	    // Create compute shader if not already created
  1902	    if (!g_StarfieldState.textCS) {
  1903	        LogToFile("[Text] Creating compute shader...");
  1904	        LogToFile("[Text] Shader bytecode size: %zu bytes", sizeof(g_KartographerTextCS));
  1905	        HRESULT hr = g_StarfieldState.device->CreateComputeShader(
  1906	            g_KartographerTextCS, sizeof(g_KartographerTextCS), nullptr, &g_StarfieldState.textCS);
  1907	        if (FAILED(hr)) {
  1908	            LogToFile("[Text] FAILED: CreateComputeShader returned 0x%08X", hr);
  1909	            context->Release();
  1910	            return;
  1911	        }
  1912	        LogToFile("[Text] Compute shader created: %p", g_StarfieldState.textCS);
  1913	    } else {
  1914	        LogToFile("[Text] Using existing compute shader: %p", g_StarfieldState.textCS);
  1915	    }
  1916	    
  1917	    // Create constant buffer if not already created
  1918	    if (!g_StarfieldState.textCB) {
  1919	        LogToFile("[Text] Creating constant buffer...");
  1920	        D3D11_BUFFER_DESC cbDesc = {};
  1921	        cbDesc.ByteWidth = sizeof(TextParams);
  1922	        cbDesc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DYNAMIC;
  1923	        cbDesc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_CONSTANT_BUFFER;
  1924	        cbDesc.CPUAccessFlags = D3D11_CPU_ACCESS_WRITE;
  1925	        HRESULT hr = g_StarfieldState.device->CreateBuffer(&cbDesc, nullptr, &g_StarfieldState.textCB);
  1926	        if (SUCCEEDED(hr)) {
  1927	            LogToFile("[Text] Constant buffer created: %p", g_StarfieldState.textCB);
  1928	        } else {
  1929	            LogToFile("[Text] WARNING: CreateBuffer for CB returned 0x%08X", hr);
  1930	        }
  1931	    } else {
  1932	        LogToFile("[Text] Using existing constant buffer: %p", g_StarfieldState.textCB);
  1933	    }
  1934	    
  1935	    // Create sampler if not already created
  1936	    // Use POINT filtering for crisp pixel fonts - LINEAR will blur the edges
  1937	    if (!g_StarfieldState.textSampler) {
  1938	        LogToFile("[Text] Creating point sampler for pixel font...");
  1939	        D3D11_SAMPLER_DESC sampDesc = {};
  1940	        sampDesc.Filter = D3D11_FILTER_MIN_MAG_MIP_POINT;
  1941	        sampDesc.AddressU = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_CLAMP;
  1942	        sampDesc.AddressV = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_CLAMP;
  1943	        sampDesc.AddressW = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_CLAMP;
  1944	        HRESULT hr = g_StarfieldState.device->CreateSamplerState(&sampDesc, &g_StarfieldState.textSampler);
  1945	        if (SUCCEEDED(hr)) {
  1946	            LogToFile("[Text] Sampler created: %p", g_StarfieldState.textSampler);
  1947	        } else {
  1948	            LogToFile("[Text] WARNING: CreateSamplerState returned 0x%08X", hr);
  1949	        }
  1950	    } else {
  1951	        LogToFile("[Text] Using existing sampler: %p", g_StarfieldState.textSampler);
  1952	    }
  1953	    
  1954	    // Create UAV for output texture
  1955	    LogToFile("[Text] Creating UAV for output texture...");
  1956	    ID3D11UnorderedAccessView* outputUAV = nullptr;
  1957	    D3D11_UNORDERED_ACCESS_VIEW_DESC uavDesc = {};
  1958	    uavDesc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM;
  1959	    uavDesc.ViewDimension = D3D11_UAV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
  1960	    HRESULT hr = g_StarfieldState.device->CreateUnorderedAccessView(outputTexture, &uavDesc, &outputUAV);
  1961	    if (FAILED(hr)) {

The math on the C# side is calculating a UV scaling factor based on the assumption of square pixels, which should theoretically result in a 1:1 mapping of texture pixels to screen pixels. 

Hmm... if the math checks out on the CPU side, the issue must lie in how the GPU is interpreting these UVs or how the vertices are being generated. The texture is 1024x1024, but the text content only occupies a small sub-rect (598x240). 

Wait, I need to look closer at the shader logic in `KartographerPS.hlsl`. The fragment shader performs a coordinate transformation: `float2 textLocal = (uv - TextOrigin) / TextAreaSize;`. This `textLocal` coordinate is then used to sample the `TextTexture`. If `TextAreaSize` is derived from the same `pixelsToUv` calculation used for the bounding box, then any error in that calculation would affect both the box and the text. However, the user says the box size is correct, which suggests the `TextAreaSize` might actually be correct for the box but somehow wrong for the text, or the sampling is failing.

But wait—if the text is appearing blurry or scaled, I should consider the vertex shader. The pixel shader can't render text if the underlying geometry doesn't cover the correct screen area. If the vertex shader is using a hardcoded or incorrect resolution to transform the glyphs, the texture mapping will be stretched regardless of the per-frame constants. 

Actually, the text rendering pipeline might involve two distinct steps: one to render the glyphs into the 1024x1024 texture (likely via a compute shader or a separate geometry pass) and another to composite that texture onto the screen using the `KartographerPS` shader. If the initial glyph rendering is using the wrong viewport or resolution settings, the texture itself will be distorted before it even reaches the compositing stage.

I need to see how the glyph positions and UVs are calculated. The `KartographerText.hlsl` file likely contains the compute shader or vertex logic that handles the initial text rendering into the texture. If the scale is wrong there, the texture data will be incorrect, and no amount of fixing the pixel shader constants will help. I need to check how it handles the `TextAreaSize` and whether it accounts for the actual texture resolution vs. the content size.

     1	// Kartographer Text Compute Shader
     2	// Scatters bitmap glyphs from atlas into a render target for sampling by KartographerPS
     3	// 
     4	// Inputs:
     5	//   t0: Atlas texture (R8_UNORM bitmap coverage)
     6	//   t1: Glyph instance structured buffer
     7	//   u0: Output texture (RGBA8 UAV)
     8	//
     9	// Constant Buffer:
    10	//   b0: TextParams (glyph count, output size)
    11	
    12	struct GlyphData
    13	{
    14	    float2 pos;      // Pixel position in text-RT space (top-left of quad)
    15	    float2 size;     // Output size in pixels
    16	    float4 uv;       // Atlas UV rect (x, y, width, height)
    17	    uint color;      // Packed ARGB
    18	    float smoothing; // unused in bitmap path
    19	};
    20	
    21	cbuffer TextParams : register(b0)
    22	{
    23	    int GlyphCount;
    24	    float2 OutputSize;
    25	    float2 Pad;
    26	};
    27	
    28	Texture2D<float> Atlas : register(t0);
    29	StructuredBuffer<GlyphData> Glyphs : register(t1);
    30	RWTexture2D<float4> Output : register(u0);
    31	
    32	SamplerState PointClamp : register(s0);
    33	
    34	// Unpack ARGB color to float4 RGBA
    35	float4 UnpackColor(uint color)
    36	{
    37	    float a = float((color >> 24) & 0xFF) / 255.0;
    38	    float r = float((color >> 16) & 0xFF) / 255.0;
    39	    float g = float((color >> 8) & 0xFF) / 255.0;
    40	    float b = float(color & 0xFF) / 255.0;
    41	    return float4(r, g, b, a);
    42	}
    43	
    44	[numthreads(64, 1, 1)]
    45	void CSMain(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID)
    46	{
    47	    uint idx = id.x;
    48	    if (idx >= (uint)GlyphCount)
    49	        return;
    50	    
    51	    GlyphData g = Glyphs[idx];
    52	    float4 col = UnpackColor(g.color);
    53	    
    54	    // Calculate pixel bounds for this glyph
    55	    int2 minPixel = int2(g.pos);
    56	    int2 maxPixel = int2(g.pos + g.size);
    57	    
    58	    // Clamp to output bounds
    59	    minPixel = clamp(minPixel, int2(0, 0), int2(OutputSize));
    60	    maxPixel = clamp(maxPixel, int2(0, 0), int2(OutputSize));
    61	    
    62	    // Rasterize glyph quad
    63	    for (int y = minPixel.y; y < maxPixel.y; y++)
    64	    {
    65	        for (int x = minPixel.x; x < maxPixel.x; x++)
    66	        {
    67	            // Sample at destination pixel center for accurate point sampling
    68	            float2 local = float2(x + 0.5, y + 0.5) - g.pos;
    69	            float2 uv = local / g.size;
    70	            
    71	            // Clamp to avoid right/bottom edge spill due to precision
    72	            uv = saturate(uv);
    73	            
    74	            // Sample bitmap atlas (coverage alpha, not SDF distance)
    75	            float2 atlasUV = float2(g.uv.x, g.uv.y) + uv * float2(g.uv.z, g.uv.w);
    76	            float alpha = Atlas.SampleLevel(PointClamp, atlasUV, 0);
    77	            
    78	            if (alpha > 0.0)
    79	            {
    80	                uint2 coord = uint2(x, y);
    81	                float4 src = col;
    82	                src.a *= alpha;
    83	                
    84	                // Alpha blend with existing content
    85	                float4 dst = Output[coord];
    86	                Output[coord] = src + dst * (1.0 - src.a);
    87	            }
    88	        }
    89	    }

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

I've identified a significant discrepancy in how the UV coordinates are calculated in `KartographerSelector.cs`. 

Looking at the logs, the measured text is 598x240 pixels. The code calculates `pixelsToUvY = 2.0f / 2160` (≈0.0009259) for a 4K screen. When converting the text dimensions to UV space, the code uses `textWidthUV = _textWidthPixels * pixelsToUvX`. If `pixelsToUvX` equals `pixelsToUvY`, this results in approximately 0.5537 for width and 0.2222 for height. 

Now, let's look at how the shader uses these values. In the shader, the UV space spans from -1 to 1, giving it a total range of 2.0. If the text is supposed to occupy 0.5537 of that range, it would cover roughly 27% of the screen width. For a 3840-pixel wide screen, that's over 1000 pixels. However, the user's measurements indicate the actual on-screen width is much smaller, suggesting the texture is being sampled with a different scaling factor or the UV coordinates are being compressed.

Hmm... the `Screen.text` calculation in the logs shows `textWidthUV * Screen.width * 0.5f`. Let's re-verify that. If `textWidthUV` is the coordinate in the [-1, 1] range, then the total screen width in those units is 2. So `textWidthUV / 2.0` is the percentage of the screen. Thus, `textWidthUV * 0.5 * Screen.width` should be the pixel count. If the code calculates `screenTextWidth` as ~1063, but the actual result is ~360, there's a major inconsistency.

Wait, I see the potential issue in the code I just read. The calculation `pixelsToUvX = pixelsToUvY` assumes square pixels. While that's standard, the relationship between screen pixels and UV space might be getting distorted if the render target dimensions used by the shader don't match `Screen.width` and `Screen.height`. 

Furthermore, the box width calculation uses the same `pixelsToUvX` logic. If the box is also the wrong size, it confirms the scaling factor itself is wrong for the target surface. But why would `2.0 / Screen.height` be wrong? It shouldn't be, unless the shader is sampling from a different coordinate system or the `TextAreaSize` is being interpreted as a normalized value (0-1) instead of a [-1, 1] value in some parts of the pipeline.

Actually, there's another possibility. The text is rendered into a texture. If the texture is 1024x1024, and the text only occupies a 598x240 portion of it, the `TextAreaSize` must correctly represent that sub-region's scale in UV space. If the shader assumes the entire 0-1 range of the texture is the text, it will stretch those 598 pixels across the whole width.

I need to check `StarfieldNative.cs`. That's likely where the native plugin interface is defined, and where the `KartographerParams` struct is marshaled. I need to see how `TextOrigin` and `TextAreaSize` are defined and passed to the native side. It's possible the native side expects these in a different coordinate system (e.g., top-left origin vs. center origin, or 0-1 range vs. -1 to 1 range).

Unless I see the exact struct layout and the marshaling attributes, I'm just guessing about the coordinate transformation. The issue is almost certainly a mismatch between the C# UV math (which assumes a specific screen-to-UV mapping) and what the native shader actually expects for the overlay. Let me examine the native bindings to clarify this.

     5	    /// <summary>
     6	    /// Shared projection math for converting world-space star directions to screen UV coordinates.

     9	    public static class KartographerMath

    14	        /// <summary>
    15	        /// Apply catalog rotation to a star direction vector.

    17	        /// HLSL rotate3D applies X, then Y, then Z.
    18	        /// </summary>
    19	        public static Vector3 ApplyCatalogRotation(Vector3 direction, float rotX, float rotY, float rotZ)
    20	        {
    21	            // Convert degrees to radians
    22	            float radX = rotX * Mathf.Deg2Rad;
    23	            float radY = rotY * Mathf.Deg2Rad;
    24	            float radZ = rotZ * Mathf.Deg2Rad;
    25	
    26	            float cosX = Mathf.Cos(radX);
    27	            float sinX = Mathf.Sin(radX);
    28	            float cosY = Mathf.Cos(radY);
    29	            float sinY = Mathf.Sin(radY);
    30	            float cosZ = Mathf.Cos(radZ);
    31	            float sinZ = Mathf.Sin(radZ);
    32	
    33	            // Rotation around X axis
    34	            Vector3 v1 = new Vector3(
    35	                direction.x,
    36	                direction.y * cosX - direction.z * sinX,
    37	                direction.y * sinX + direction.z * cosX
    38	            );
    39	
    40	            // Rotation around Y axis
    41	            Vector3 v2 = new Vector3(
    42	                v1.x * cosY + v1.z * sinY,
    43	                v1.y,
    44	                -v1.x * sinY + v1.z * cosY
    45	            );
    46	
    47	            // Rotation around Z axis
    48	            Vector3 v3 = new Vector3(
    49	                v2.x * cosZ - v2.y * sinZ,
    50	                v2.x * sinZ + v2.y * cosZ,
    51	                v2.z
    52	            );
    53	
    54	            return v3.normalized;
    55	        }
    56	
    57	        /// <summary>
    58	        /// Projects a world-space direction vector to screen UV coordinates (0-1 range).
    59	        /// Returns (-1, -1) if the star is behind the camera.
    60	        /// </summary>
    61	        public static Vector2 WorldDirectionToScreenUV(
    62	            Vector3 worldDir,
    63	            Vector3 cameraRight,
    64	            Vector3 cameraUp,
    65	            Vector3 cameraForward,
    66	            float aspect,
    67	            float verticalFOV)
    68	        {
    69	            // Inverse of ViewToWorld from KartographerPS.hlsl:
    70	            // ViewToWorld: world = v.x * right - v.y * up + v.z * forward
    71	            // So view.x = dot(world, right)
    72	            //     view.y = -dot(world, up)
    73	            //     view.z = dot(world, forward)
    74	
    75	            float vx = Vector3.Dot(worldDir, cameraRight);
    76	            float vy = -Vector3.Dot(worldDir, cameraUp);
    77	            float vz = Vector3.Dot(worldDir, cameraForward);
    78	
    79	            // Behind camera check
    80	            if (vz <= 0.001f)
    81	                return new Vector2(-1f, -1f);
    82	
    83	            // Perspective projection to NDC
    84	            // Shader: ray = normalize(float3(uv.x, uv.y, focalLength))
    85	            // So: ndcX = (vx / vz) * focalLength
    86	            //     ndcY = (vy / vz) * focalLength
    87	            float focalLength = 1.0f / Mathf.Tan(verticalFOV * 0.5f);
    88	            float ndcX = (vx / vz) * focalLength;
    89	            float ndcY = (vy / vz) * focalLength;
    90	
    91	            // NDC (-1 to 1) to UV (0 to 1)
    92	            // Shader UV construction:
    93	            //   uv.x = (input.uv.x - 0.5) * 2.0 * aspect  →  input.uv.x = uv.x / (2*aspect) + 0.5
    94	            //   uv.y = (input.uv.y - 0.5) * 2.0          →  input.uv.y = uv.y / 2 + 0.5
    95	            float u = ndcX / (2f * aspect) + 0.5f;
    96	            float v = ndcY / 2f + 0.5f;
    97	
    98	            return new Vector2(u, v);
    99	        }

`pixelsToUvX = pixelsToUvY`

Now I understand the issue. Let me analyze the problem:

**Root Cause Analysis:**

The problem is that the shader UV space Y spans [-1, 1] (total range of 2), but the X spans [-aspect, aspect] (total range of 2*aspect). When you calculate `pixelsToUvX = pixelsToUvY` (both based on height), you're creating UV dimensions that don't account for the shader's aspect ratio scaling on the X axis.

In the shader:
```hlsl
float aspect = Resolution.x / Resolution.y;  // ~1.778 for 4K
float2 uv = float2(
    (input.uv.x - 0.5) * 2.0 * aspect,  // X spans [-1.778, 1.778] = 3.556 total
    (input.uv.y - 0.5) * 2.0           // Y spans [-1, 1] = 2.0 total
);
```

The shader's X coordinate is scaled by `aspect`, so a UV size of 0.5537 in X actually covers `0.5537 * (screen_width/2) / aspect` pixels, not `0.5537 * (screen_width/2)` pixels.

**The Fix:**

The text UV size needs to be calculated in the shader's coordinate space. For 1:1 pixel mapping:
- Y UV size: `pixelCount * (2.0 / screenHeight)`  
- X UV size: `pixelCount * (2.0 / screenHeight) / aspect` (because shader X is stretched by aspect)

Or simpler: just use the same scale factor for both, but divide the X size by aspect.

Here's the fix for `KartographerSelector.cs`:

#### Tool Call: StrReplaceFile (`Core/KartographerSelector.cs`)
<!-- call_id: tool_1HSA2NEsw8s6FBWXOJQdswYP -->
```json
{
  "path": "Core/KartographerSelector.cs",
  "edit": {
    "old": "            // Calculate UV conversion for 1:1 pixel mapping (square pixels)\n            // Shader UV space: X=[-aspect, aspect], Y=[-1, 1]\n            // For square pixels, use same scale factor for X and Y based on height\n            float pixelsToUvY = 2.0f / Screen.height;                    // base scale on height\n            float pixelsToUvX = pixelsToUvY;                             // same scale for square pixels\n            \n            // Box size: text bounds + padding on sides/top, extra padding on bottom\n            // pixelsToUv already includes aspect ratio, no extra multiply needed\n            float boxWidthUV = (_textWidthPixels + BOX_PADDING_PIXELS * 2) * pixelsToUvX;\n            float boxHeightUV = (_textHeightPixels + BOX_PADDING_PIXELS + BOX_PADDING_BOTTOM_PIXELS) * pixelsToUvY;",
    "new": "            // Calculate UV conversion for 1:1 pixel mapping (square pixels)\n            // Shader UV space: X=[-aspect, aspect], Y=[-1, 1]\n            // The shader scales X by aspect: uv.x = (input.uv.x - 0.5) * 2.0 * aspect\n            // So to get 1:1 pixels, we need to divide X UV size by aspect\n            float pixelsToUvY = 2.0f / Screen.height;                    // base scale on height\n            float pixelsToUvX = pixelsToUvY / AspectRatio;               // divide by aspect for X\n            \n            // Box size: text bounds + padding on sides/top, extra padding on bottom\n            float boxWidthUV = (_textWidthPixels + BOX_PADDING_PIXELS * 2) * pixelsToUvX;\n            float boxHeightUV = (_textHeightPixels + BOX_PADDING_PIXELS + BOX_PADDING_BOTTOM_PIXELS) * pixelsToUvY;"
  }
}
```

   126	                _starfieldRenderBuffer = null;
   127	            }

   130	        // Cached reflection info for GalaxyCubeControl.glareFade (private field)
   131	        private static System.Reflection.FieldInfo _glareFadeField = null;
   132	        private static bool _glareFadeReflectionAttempted = false;
   133	
   134	        /// <summary>
   135	        /// Calculate dimming factor from sun glare (GalaxyCubeControl.glareFade).
   136	        /// Returns 1.0 (full brightness) when sun not in view, down to 0.2f minimum when sun centered.
   137	        /// Note: glareFade is a private field, accessed via cached reflection.
   138	        /// </summary>
   139	        private float GetSunGlareDimming()
   140	        {
   141	            if (GalaxyCubeControl.Instance == null)
   142	                return 1.0f;
   143	
   144	            if (!_glareFadeReflectionAttempted)
   145	            {
   146	                try
   147	                {
   148	                    _glareFadeField = typeof(GalaxyCubeControl).GetField("glareFade",
   149	                        System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Instance);
   150	                }
   151	                catch { _glareFadeField = null; }
   152	                _glareFadeReflectionAttempted = true;
   153	            }
   154	
   155	            if (_glareFadeField == null) return 1.0f;
   156	
   157	            try
   158	            {
   159	                float glare = (float)_glareFadeField.GetValue(GalaxyCubeControl.Instance);
   160	                if (float.IsNaN(glare) || float.IsInfinity(glare)) return 1.0f;
   161	
   162	                // Aggressive drop-off, then floor at 0.1
   163	                // glareFade 0.0 → 1.0 (full stars)
   164	                // glareFade 0.6 → ~0.15 (aggressive dimming)  
   165	                // glareFade 1.0 → 0.1 (floor)
   166	                float curvedDimming = 1.0f - Mathf.Pow(glare, 0.3f);
   167	
   168	                const float MIN_BRIGHTNESS = 0.1f;
   169	                return Mathf.Max(curvedDimming, MIN_BRIGHTNESS);
   170	            }
   171	            catch { return 1.0f; }
   172	        }
   173	
   174	        /// <summary>
   175	        /// Calculate planetary dimming - FLIGHT ONLY with distance early-out.
   176	        /// Skips bodies > 1 billion meters (1 million km) away.
   177	        /// </summary>
   178	        private float CalculatePlanetaryDimming(Camera cam)
   179	        {
   180	            // Only calculate in Flight view - coordinates are consistent here
   181	            if (HighLogic.LoadedScene != GameScenes.FLIGHT)
   182	                return 1.0f;
   183	
   184	            if (cam == null || FlightGlobals.Bodies == null || FlightGlobals.Bodies.Count == 0)
   185	                return 1.0f;
   186	
   187	            Vector3d camPos = cam.transform.position;
   188	            Vector3d camForward = cam.transform.forward;
   189	            double camFov = cam.fieldOfView;
   190	
   191	            double minDimming = 1.0;
   192	            const double MIN_BRIGHTNESS = 0.25;  // Planets never dim below 25%
   193	            const double MIN_ANGULAR_SIZE = 0.5;  // degrees
   194	            const double REFERENCE_BODY_SIZE = 2.0;  // degrees
   195	            const double MIN_TARGET_REL_ANGLE = 90.0;  // degrees
   196	            const double MAX_DISTANCE_SQR = 1e18; // (1,000,000,000 meters)^2
   197	
   198	            CelestialBody sun = FlightGlobals.Bodies[0];
   199	
   200	            for (int i = 1; i < FlightGlobals.Bodies.Count; i++)
   201	            {
   202	                CelestialBody body = FlightGlobals.Bodies[i];
   203	                if (body == null || body == sun) continue;
   204	
   205	                // EARLY OUT: Skip bodies > 1 billion meters away (1 million km)
   206	                Vector3d offset = body.position - camPos;
   207	                if (offset.sqrMagnitude > MAX_DISTANCE_SQR)
   208	                    continue;
   209	
   210	                // Now do the math only for nearby bodies
   211	                double dist = body.GetAltitude(camPos) + body.Radius;
   212	                double radius = body.Radius;
   213	
   214	                double bodyAngularSize = Math.Atan2(radius, dist) * (180.0 / Math.PI);
   215	
   216	                if (bodyAngularSize < MIN_ANGULAR_SIZE)
   217	                    continue;
   218	
   219	                // Phase calculation: angle between body->sun and body->camera
   220	                Vector3d bodyPos = body.position;
   221	                Vector3d bodyToSun = sun.position - bodyPos;
   222	                Vector3d bodyToCam = camPos - bodyPos;
   223	
   224	                double phaseAngle = Vector3d.Angle(bodyToSun, bodyToCam);
   225	                phaseAngle = Math.Max(phaseAngle, bodyAngularSize);
   226	                phaseAngle = Math.Min(phaseAngle, MIN_TARGET_REL_ANGLE);
   227	                double phaseFactor = 1.0 - ((phaseAngle - bodyAngularSize) / (MIN_TARGET_REL_ANGLE - bodyAngularSize));
   228	
   229	                // View alignment: how centered in camera?
   230	                double viewAngle = Math.Max(0.0, Vector3.Angle((bodyPos - camPos).normalized, camForward) - bodyAngularSize);
   231	                double viewAlignment = 1.0 - Math.Min(1.0, Math.Max(0.0, (viewAngle - (camFov / 2.0)) - 5.0) / (camFov / 4.0));
   232	
   233	                // Size weight
   234	                double sizeWeight = Math.Sqrt(Math.Min(bodyAngularSize, REFERENCE_BODY_SIZE) / REFERENCE_BODY_SIZE);
   235	
   236	                // Calculate dimming for this body
   237	                double bodyDimming = 1.0 - (phaseFactor * sizeWeight * viewAlignment);
   238	
   239	                if (bodyDimming < minDimming)
   240	                {
   241	                    minDimming = bodyDimming;
   242	                    if (minDimming <= MIN_BRIGHTNESS)
   243	                    {
   244	                        minDimming = MIN_BRIGHTNESS;
   245	                        break;
   246	                    }
   247	                }
   248	            }
   249	
   250	            return (float)Math.Max(minDimming, MIN_BRIGHTNESS);
   251	        }

Build successful. Now the actual fix is in place:

**The Real Fix:**

The shader multiplies X coordinates by aspect ratio internally:
```hlsl
uv.x = (input.uv.x - 0.5) * 2.0 * aspect
```

So when we pass `TextAreaSizeX` to the shader, it gets stretched by aspect. To compensate and achieve 1:1 pixel mapping:

- **Before:** `pixelsToUvX = 2.0 / screenHeight` → UV width = 598 × 0.000926 = **0.5537**
- **After:** `pixelsToUvX = (2.0 / screenHeight) / aspect` → UV width = 598 × 0.000926 / 1.778 = **0.311**

With the new UV width of 0.311, when the shader multiplies by aspect (1.778), the effective coverage becomes 0.5537 in the stretched space, which correctly maps to 598 screen pixels.

- UV text: ~0.311 x 0.2222
- Screen text: ~598 x 240 pixels (actual on-screen measurement)

## The Real Problem
The shader samples the text texture using:
```hlsl
float2 textLocal = (uv - TextOrigin) / TextAreaSize;
float coverage = TextTexture.SampleLevel(TextSampler, textLocal, 0).r;
```

`TextAreaSize` maps shader UV space to texture UV space [0,1]. The issue is:

```hlsl
uv.x = (input.uv.x - 0.5) * 2.0 * aspect  // X is stretched by aspect!
```

So the same UV value covers different amounts of screen space in X vs Y!

- Changed from using `_textWidthPixels * pixelsToUv` (598 × 0.000926 = 0.5537) 
- To using `1024 * pixelsToUv` (1024 × 0.000926 = 0.9481)

**Expected result:**
- UV text size: ~0.948 x 0.948
- On-screen size: ~1024 x 1024 pixels at 4K
- The 1024x1024 texture drawn 1:1 with the letter "N" at 22x28 pixels (not squished)
